Applied 64 EXTRACTED-tier and INFERRED-tier wikilinks across 15 pages: (a) d4/d5/d7 → d60 backlink (extract-vs-full-Misrep hypothesis; d60 verifies Arabian Gulf filename at internal-coord level); (b) d8 → d60 backlink (thermal-polarity contrast); (c) d50/d52 → d60 backlink (PAROC/IDAT triple-attestation: d50 12 AF/DET 3 + d52 15 AF/DET 1 + d60 432 AEW/482ATKS Active QC; d60 resolves d52 PAROL↔PAROC ambiguity in favor of PAROC); (d) d54 → d60 backlink (inverse pattern: morphology-rich-thin-extract vs morphology-blank-rich-Misrep-parent); (e) d55 → d56/d38/d60 backlinks (Block A release-framework family + first internal Navy user via d56 P-8A → MH-60R extension); (f) d58 → d38/d56/d60 backlinks (range-fouler N=4 anchor + d58 capability-triad isolated at 1-of-4 + d60 closes 000001 question); (g) d44 → d38/d56/d60 backlinks (paired Arabian-Sea cluster with d56; d38 carries distinct Block B; d60 same MQ-9 platform + Block A); (h) entities/usaf.md added Modern USAF UAP-reporting anchors section listing 48FW/15 AF/12 AF/77 EFS/172 ATKS/482ATKS/609 CAOC with d60 PAROC/IDAT triple-attestation; (i) entities/aaro.md added AARO as receiving authority section with N=6 stamp recurrence + Block A/Block B split note; (j) references/dow-uap-pr20-prepublication-clearance-2026-03 added full Block A/Block B explanation against PR20 DOPSR clearance date positioning between the two release dates; (k) concepts/orb-phenomenon added d8/d38/d44/d56 backlinks (round morphology in modern dow-uap corpus); (l) synthesis/uap-phenomenon-nature added CENTCOM-AOR maritime corridor cluster + Arabian Gulf internal-coordinate verification at d60. AMBIGUOUS-tier candidates not applied: tables in concepts/range-fouler.md inline d38/d44/d56/d58 abbreviation references (e.g. `| d58 |` cells) — would clutter analytical tables; already fully linked at top-anchor + See also level. THREAD STUB CANDIDATES (12 — see thread_stub_candidates in report): 432-aew, 482atks (Active MQ-9), hsm-73 (Navy Battlecats), mh-60r-seahawk (Romeo), ali-al-salem-ab-okas (Kuwait launch), navcent, 609-caoc (USCENTCOM CAOC Al Udeid), dgs1 (Langley AFB PED), airhandler (SIGINT payload), iranian-air-defense, fmv-method, paroc-idat-role — all attested across multiple d* ingests. PAROC/IDAT, NAVCENT, 432 AEW, HSM-73, MH-60R, 609 CAOC are highest-fanout (each appears in 2-3+ d* pages). BROKEN TARGETS: 0 new broken targets introduced; 26 carryover intentional forward-placeholders unchanged from batch-5 (per TODO.md Known broken-link forward placeholders list). MISC: no misc/ directory in vault → misc_affinity_updated=0 / promotion_candidates=0 (skipped per scope). STATE FILES UPDATED: 15 modified content pages + log.md + hot.md (no manifest update — cross-linker doesn't touch manifest). KEY CROSS-CUTTING FINDINGS: (1) Block A 5-of-6 release-block dominance now visible across the corpus after d60's sequential 000001→000006 6-page stamping closes the 000001 ambiguity; (2) d44+d56 paired-Arabian-Sea sub-theater cluster (52 days, both Block A, both passive maritime surveillance) emerges as a NAVCENT/5th Fleet sub-cluster independent of Block split; (3) PAROC/IDAT triple-attestation across 3 different USAF units (15 AF / DET 1, 12 AF / DET 3, 432 AEW/482ATKS Active) qualifies as a cross-source corpus pattern; (4) d60's Iranian Air Defense GUARDCALL is the first foreign-state direct hail in dow-uap — flagged as synthesis candidate for future wiki-synthesize pass; (5) advisor-noted bias holds: ingest-time linking writes outward to hubs; old-page→new-page backlinking pressure concentrates on sibling pages with stale See also sections."

AARO-receiving-authority firms further at N=6 stamp-recurrence. **FILENAME-THEATER VERIFIES AT INTERNAL-COORDINATE LEVEL** — 5-of-5 internal MGRS coords (UTM zones 39R + 40R) decode inside the body-text-declared `IVO ARABIAN GULF, STRAIT OF HORMUZ AND GULF OF OMAN` operational area; first `*-arabian-*` / `*-persian-gulf*`-class filename verification at internal-coord level; revises d4/d5/d7 'Arabian Gulf'-mismatch hypothesis (extract-vs-full-Misrep reading most coherent — parent Misreps may decode correctly while single-segment UAP-event extracts diverge from parent AOR). (b) first explicit Misrep identifier (Misrep 4592219); (c) first multi-page release-sequence-stamping (000001 → 000006 sequential); (d) first `GENTEXT/OBSERVATION` segment for UAP datum (vs prior `GENTEXT/UAP` extract header); (e) first explicit USAF unit attribution for mission report (482ATKS / 432 AEW / 603 AOC); (f) first operations-center attribution (603 AOC + 609th AOC + 609 CAOC — three tokens); (g) first 609 CAOC (Combined Air Operations Center, Al Udeid AB Qatar ^[inferred]); (bb) first mission-report UAP-datum count increment to N=6 records / 7 datums; (cc) first filename-axis verification at internal-coordinate level for `*-arabian-*` / `*-persian-gulf*`-class filename — 5-of-5 internal MGRS coords decode inside body-text operational area; (dd) first explicit USAF Active mission-report unit anchor (multi-service count 7→8). **EIGHTH DISTINCT BRIEF-OBSERVATION SUB-CLASS** anchored on concepts/uap-aircraft-engagement: *FMV-observation + zero-mission-impact + kinematics-blank + morphology-blank* (signature is **bare ISR-logged observation** with full operational context around it; Per-batch sequence-number reading firms decisively at N=5 (4 documents share `000001` in Block A vs `001` in Block B); OCR-artifact reading definitively rejected at N=5. **FORM-TITLE at N=4: 3 Debrief + 1 Reporting** — d56 Debrief matches d38+d58; d44 remains lone Reporting attestation. Date-order Debrief→Debrief→Reporting→Debrief still non-monotonic; form-version-evolution reading still rejected; two-co-existing-form-variants reading firms most-probable; Debrief is now dominant variant. **PASSIVE SUB-CLASS B FIRMS to 3-of-4 DOMINANT** — d56+d44+d38 = passive non-intercept observation; d58 intercept-posture isolated at 1-of-4 within range-fouler-debrief class. Sub-class B is now anchored across **two platform configurations** (fixed-wing ISR via d38+d44 + rotary-wing maritime SSC via d56). **d58 CAPABILITY TRIAD ISOLATED at 1-of-4** — sub-frame disappearance + active EW + 16.9 NM standoff all isolated to d58 attestation; d58 firms as the high-anomaly outlier in the range-fouler class. **CENTCOM-AOR anchoring at 100% (4-of-4)** — all four range-fouler-debrief events fall in CENTCOM AOR. **STRUCTURAL FIRSTS (~30 listed on d56 reference page)**: (a) fourth range-fouler-debrief artifact; (b) first internal Navy user anchor in range-fouler subset (HSM-73 Battlecats); (c) first Sikorsky MH-60R Romeo Seahawk platform anchor in dow-uap corpus + wider UAP wiki corpus; (d) first helicopter / rotary-wing witnessing platform in dow-uap corpus; (e) first explicit Side No. + Buno values captured in range-fouler class; (f) first SSC (Surface Search and Control) mission descriptor in dow-uap corpus; (g) first dual-morphology checkbox (Round + Wings/Airframe); (h) first Wings/Airframe morphology attestation in class; (i) first 3-contact group (group-size distribution at N=4 now 1+1+2+3 — full distribution); (j) first explicit `No` Stable-Trackfile value; (k) first explicit triple-negative passive-sensor datum (ES + radar + IFF all negative); (l) first explicit Wind Direction (310°) + Wind Speed (5 KT) values in class; (m) first explicit `Pilot` crew-position value in class; (n) first explicit `UNK` speed value in class; (o) first explicit formation-flying multi-object kinematic attestation in dow-uap corpus (`maintain relative course, speed, and altitude`); (p) first lost-then-regained-with-additional-contacts narrative pattern; (q) first explicit no-interaction narrative attestation in corpus (strengthens d50 tearline `NO INTERFERENCE WAS NOTED` at witness-narrative level); (r) first explicit North Arabian Sea body-text anchor in corpus — sister to d44 Gulf of Aden in Arabian-Sea sub-theater paired-incident cluster (52 days apart, both passive Navy/USAF maritime surveillance, both Block A); (s) first explicit `small` size descriptor; (t) Block A firms to 4-of-5 dominant; (u) per-batch sequence-number reading firms decisively at N=5; (v) third 2-of-2 filename-axis-internally-verified artifact (`arabian-sea` ↔ North Arabian Sea + `august-2020` ↔ `08/24/20`); (w) EA Indications block structurally anchored at N=3 (all-unchecked across d44+d38+d56); (x) 4-of-4 SPEAR sanitization-clause attestation; (y) Round morphology dominant at 3-of-4 within range-fouler class; (z) `range fouler` institutional-vocabulary reading firms at N=3-of-4 (only d58 uses term in narrative); (aa) passive sub-class B anchored at 3-of-4 dominant + extends to rotary-wing platform configuration. **CLOSURES**: (1) range-fouler class characterisation FIRMED at N=4 ^closed-by-dow-uap-d56 (anchor + validation + extension + N=4 confirmation); Non-monotonic sequence (Debrief → Reporting → Debrief) falsifies the form-version-evolution reading committed at the d44 ingest. Two-co-existing-form-variants reading is most probable at N=3 ^[inferred]; distribution = 2 Debrief + 1 Reporting. **CRITICAL FINDING — RELEASE-BLOCK DIVERGENCE**: d38 carries Block B (`Declassified by MG Richard A. Harrison` + `USCENTCOM MDR 26-0019` single case + `Approved for Release to AARO` + `01/26/26 001` 3-digit sequence) — **distinct from d55+d58+d44's Block A** (`MDR 26-0038 to 26-0046` 9-case range + `03/27/26 000001` 6-digit sequence, 60 days later release date). **THE 'BYTE-FOR-BYTE RELEASE-BLOCK RECURRENCE AT N=3' FINDING IS PARTIAL AT N=4** of release-block-testable artifacts. The dow-uap range-fouler+mission-record subset spans **at least 2 distinct USCENTCOM MDR allocations**; the release framework is per-MDR-case-allocation, not per-tranche-uniform ^[inferred]. **Per-batch sequence-number reading strengthens decisively** at N=4 (001 in one batch + 000001 in another rejects OCR-artifact reading). **d58 CAPABILITY TRIAD ISOLATED AT 1-of-3**: d58's headline sub-frame disappearance + active noise jamming + 16.9 NM standoff is now isolated within the range-fouler-debrief class; passive-ISR-observation sub-class (d44 + d38, both single-contact IR-cold round-morphology on Black-Hot polarity) is **dominant at 2-of-3** within the class. **STRUCTURAL FIRSTS (25 listed on d38 reference page)**: (a) earliest range-fouler in corpus (May 2020 anchor); (i) first explicit sensor-mode string (ULTN/Black Hot/Lin); (j) first explicit sensor-operator + crew-structure attestation in dow-uap corpus narrative; (k) first explicit sensor-tracking-effort attestation (water-waves visibility-from-operator-slewing); two-variants reading most probable); (4) d58 capability-triad-recurrence question CLOSED at N=3 negative (d58 isolated at 1-of-3); (5) passive-ISR-observation sub-class recurrence question CLOSED at N=2 (sub-class B dominant); - [2026-05-11T22:00:00Z] INGEST source="sources/dow-uap-d44-range-fouler-arabian-sea-october-2020.json" pages_created=1 pages_updated=2 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official notes="1-page Mistral-OCR'd Range Fouler Reporting Form (3,588 bytes) — ELEVENTH artifact in the DoW-UAP FOIA release series (11-of-40); SECOND range-fouler debrief artifact and N=2 VALIDATION of the document class anchored at N=1 by d58. 15 Oct 2020 1418:39Z-to-1419:52Z daytime Gulf of Aden encounter (12 days BEFORE d58), 172 ATKS user (Michigan Air National Guard 172d Attack Squadron, Battle Creek MI, MQ-9 Reaper community ^[inferred]); 1X round IR-cold (bright-white on black-hot polarity) contact tracked passively via MTS-B IR turret for 73 seconds; slant range 4.06 NM, ground range 4.78 km, sensor angle -50° below platform; abrupt directional changes during bounded track (form: 261°/30 mph; narrative: 319°/20 mph — most-coherent reading is two snapshots within the 73-sec track); zero EA reception (all 5 EA Indications boxes unchecked — first explicit Electronic Attack reception schema captured in corpus); MGRS location 40Q BD (Arabian Peninsula southern grid / Gulf of Aden waters); 19,073 HAT platform altitude; sensor-geometry derivation puts contact altitude at ~140 ft HAT over sea surface ^[inferred]. **N=2 VALIDATION OUTCOME — CONFIRMED on structural axes, DIVERGES on capability-signature axes**: 11-of-11 testable schema axes MATCH (instruction block + identity grid + position grid + sensor+track grid + EA Indications block — novel at N=2 — + 12-checkbox morphology grid + narrative block + submission template + SPEAR clause + release framework + filename token honesty). Title VARIANT — d44 = `# Range Fouler Reporting Form`; d58 = `# Range Fouler Debrief Form` ^[ambiguous]; three readings (form-version evolution most probable given 12-day spread, OCR variance weak, two co-existing variants plausible). Capability-signature DIVERGES — d58's headline triad (1/30-s sub-frame disappearance + active noise jamming + 16.9 NM standoff maintenance) is d58-isolated at N=2; d44 is bounded 73-sec passive observation with no jamming, single contact, daytime, abrupt directional changes but no sub-second exit, IR-cold (no propulsion heat plume) low-altitude over-water — readable as kite-class / drone-class / controlled balloon / anomalous-but-not-extraordinary. Class catalogs the FULL range-intrusion distribution; d58 high-anomaly tail, d44 mid-distribution. **RELEASE-BLOCK RECURRENCE AT N=3**: byte-for-byte identical Harrison declassification + USCENTCOM MDR 26-0038 to MDR 26-0046 + Approved for Release to AARO + 03/27/26 + 000001 across d55 + d58 + d44. Firms AARO-receiving-authority closure further; weakens OCR-artifact reading of 000001 (three independent OCR passes producing identical corruption implausible); strengthens per-batch reading. **CENTCOM-AOR ANCHORING firms to N=2**: d44 explicit Gulf of Aden + MGRS 40Q BD body anchor; d58 inferred via MDR + 77 EFS + KINGPIN. Paired-incident hypothesis at OPERATIONAL-AREA level supported (both Oct 2020 CENTCOM AOR) but not same-object level (d44 single-IR-cold-round vs d58 two-metallic-balloon-strobe-bearing). **STRUCTURAL FIRSTS (25)**: (a) second range-fouler debrief artifact / N=2 class validation; (b) form-title variant; (c) EA Indications block — 5-row pre-printed Electronic-Attack-reception schema (ECM/Arc/Letter Identifier/False Trackfiles/Other-Ambiguous); (d) first UAV / RPA platform; (e) first non-fighter excepting d55 P-8A maritime patrol; (f) first Air National Guard unit anchor (172 ATKS); (g) first explicit MGRS coordinate in body text for range-fouler artifact; (h) first explicit Gulf of Aden anchor + first CENTCOM-AOR body-text anchor; (i) first HAT altitude reference; (j) first IR-cold morphology call (vs d8's WHITE HOT polarity contrast); (k) first explicit start/end encounter timestamps (73-sec bounded duration); (l) first IR-only sensor channel datum; (m) first sensor-geometry datum (slant + ground + angle enabling vertical-separation derivation); (n) first low-altitude over-water contact derivation; (o) first range-fouler-class single-contact datum; (p) first range-fouler-class daytime datum; (q) first range-fouler-class abrupt-directional-changes kinematic; (r) first passive-ISR-observation posture in dow-uap corpus; (s) seventh distinct behavioral sub-class — range-fouler-passive-ISR-observation; (t) first explicit unit-mismatch between platform-sensor schema and form pre-print (MQ-9 lacks AIM-9X + ATFLIR; form blank values honest); (u) third witness with 'Other' crew position — reading: MQ-9 Sensor Operator; (v) release framework recurrence at N=3 (d55+d58+d44); (w) first 2-of-2 filename-axis-internally-verified artifact (arabian-sea + october-2020 both verify); (x) first evidence 'range fouler' is institutional-form vocabulary not pilot vernacular (d44 narrative uses generic object/contact only); (y) first range-fouler-class zero-EA-reception datum. **MULTI-SERVICE ANCHOR COUNT NOW 6 at N=11**: 5 USAF (4 Active + 1 ANG — 15 AF / 12 AF / 48FW / 77 EFS + 172 ATKS) + 1 Navy + Navy-aviation form-template attribution. Joint-service-use reading extends from 2 to 3 components (Navy form + Active USAF + ANG USAF). **CLOSURES**: (1) range-fouler debrief class characterisation FIRMED at N=2 (^closed-by-dow-uap-d44 anchor); (2) d44 forward-anchor on entities/dow-uap-foia-release closed; (3) 'validate the d58 range-fouler debrief class at N=2' open question CLOSED with structure-CONFIRMED / capability-DIVERGES outcome. **MANIFEST DISCIPLINE**: new source entry, no duplicate; stats.total_pages 327 → 328 (d44 reference page; concept range-fouler.md exists from d58 ingest and updated in place; entities/dow-uap-foia-release.md updated in place); stats.total_sources_ingested 56 → 57. **OPEN**: validate form-title variant at N≥3 (resolve Reporting-vs-Debrief reading); confirm 172 ATKS MQ-9 platform reading via open-source rotation history; test MTS-B sensor-channel vocabulary recurrence at N≥3; recover SPEAR program identification; test paired-incident hypothesis at operational-area level (bullseye ZIM vs MGRS 40Q BD grid mapping); test 9-document USCENTCOM-MDR sub-batch hypothesis at N≥4; resolve per-batch vs per-MDR-case 000001 reading at N≥4; validate IR-cold morphology call at N≥3; test passive-ISR-observation posture recurrence at N≥3; resolve unit-mismatch handling pattern. pages_created=[references/dow-uap-d44-range-fouler-arabian-sea-october-2020.md]. pages_updated=[entities/dow-uap-foia-release.md, concepts/range-fouler.md, index.md, timeline.md, log.md, hot.md]."
- [2026-05-11T21:00:00Z] INGEST source="sources/dow-uap-d58-range-fouler-debrief-na-october-2020.json" pages_created=2 pages_updated=2 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official notes="1-page Mistral-OCR'd Range Fouler Debrief Form (3,416 bytes) — TENTH artifact in the DoW-UAP FOIA release series (10-of-40); FIRST range-fouler debrief document class anchor in the corpus; FIRST fielded-form artifact. 27 Oct 2020 0112:21Z night DCA encounter, 77 EFS user (USAF Expeditionary Fighter Squadron — provisional fourth USAF unit anchor in the series), KINGPIN-directed intercept ID; 2X balloon-shaped metallic-opaque-reflective UAPs with red-blinking-strobe visual signature, slow eastward drift at 060°/20 KT, one circling around the other; radar lock (Other-class radar) + TGT-pod video (2 IR-significant contacts on pod); 16.9 NM minimum closest-approach floor; disappearance in 1/30 second (one TGT-pod video frame at 30 fps inferred); noise jamming received (two-chevron RWR display convention inferred — first active EW emission attributed to UAPs in the corpus); first detection at B/E ZIM 248/17 (bullseye-relative bearing 248° at range 17 NM — first bullseye-coordinate datum in the corpus). MAJOR FINDING — release-block twin to d55: d58's bottom-of-page release block is BYTE-FOR-BYTE IDENTICAL to d55's on 5 axes (Harrison declassification authority + USCENTCOM MDR 26-0038 to MDR 26-0046 + Approved for Release to AARO + 03/27/26 release-stamp date + 000001 release-sequence number). FIRMS THE AARO-RECEIVING-AUTHORITY CLOSURE at N=2 stamp-recurrence (^closed-by-dow-uap-d58 recurrence anchor on top of d55's initial closure on entities/dow-uap-foia-release.md). PARTIAL RESOLUTION of the MDR-scope question: 2-of-2 testable artifacts (2016 P-8A Syria CTG-narrative + 2020 77 EFS CENTCOM range-fouler) carry the identical 9-case MDR range; provisional hypothesis is a USCENTCOM-AOR sub-batch within the larger tranche. AMBIGUITY: identical 000001 release-sequence number on two different documents — per-MDR-case vs per-batch vs OCR-artifact reading. STRUCTURAL FIRSTS (20+): (a) first range-fouler debrief artifact (anchors third top-level mission-record document class); (b) first fielded-form artifact; (c) first explicit data-pipeline name (SPEAR — Navy-aviation centralized intake/sanitization); (d) first explicit Navy-aviation FRS template attribution (VFA-106 HUD.wmv example filename in submission instructions); (e) first explicit pre-printed Navy-aviation sensor option set (AIM-9x Self-Track + ATFLIR Autotrack checkboxes); (f) first joint-service form-template-vs-user attribution split (Navy form + USAF user); (g) provisional fourth USAF unit anchor (77 EFS); (h) first KINGPIN tactical-controller callsign; (i) first DCA mission classification; (j) first explicit night-time encounter; (k) first explicit body-text calendar date in a 2020-event artifact (10/27/20); (l) first multi-object datum with intra-group kinematic interaction (one circling the other); (m) first sub-second-timescale disappearance (1/30 s = TGT-pod inter-frame at 30 fps); (n) first electronic-warfare reception event (noise jamming, two-chevron RWR display); (o) first red-blinking-strobe morphology call; (p) first explicit closest-approach floor (16.9 NM); (q) first bullseye-coordinate datum (B/E ZIM 248/17); (r) first explicit class-abbreviation in a filename schema (_RF in Date_Squadron_RF.pdf); (s) second filename-date-internally-verified artifact (filename october-2020 ↔ body 10/27/20 — after d55); (t) sixth distinct behavioral sub-class in the corpus (range-fouler-attempted-ID). BEHAVIORAL: d58 anchors a new sixth brief-observation sub-class on concepts/uap-aircraft-engagement.md — range-fouler-attempted-ID (radar-lock + TGT-pod-video-confirmed multi-object + intra-group interaction + sub-second disappearance + active EW + KINGPIN-directed intercept + 16.9 NM standoff). Sits between d4/d5/d8 brief-observation kinematic-anomaly and d7 fire-control pipeline. STRONGEST UAP-side capability signature in the dow-uap corpus to date: radar evasion + active-EW emission + active standoff maintenance simultaneously (all ^[inferred]). CLOSURES: (1) AARO-receiving-authority closure firms from partial-pending-recurrence to confirmed at N=2 (^closed-by-dow-uap-d58); (2) range-fouler debrief document-class characterisation open question on entities/dow-uap-foia-release closed at N=1 template anchor (^closed-by-dow-uap-d58). MANIFEST DISCIPLINE: new source entry, no duplicate; stats.total_pages 325 → 327; stats.total_sources_ingested 55 → 56. OPEN: validate range-fouler debrief class at N=2 (d44 next in queue, also October 2020 — paired-incident hypothesis); resolve SPEAR program identification; resolve KINGPIN controller designator; resolve 000001 release-sequence-number identicality reading; test multi-document MDR-coverage hypothesis; test 1/30-s disappearance + noise-jamming pattern recurrence. NOTE on 77 EFS service attribution: USAF Expeditionary Fighter Squadron reading is provisional; joint-service-form-with-USAF-user reading is operationally most coherent. pages_created=[references/dow-uap-d58-range-fouler-debrief-2020-10.md, concepts/range-fouler.md]. pages_updated=[entities/dow-uap-foia-release.md, concepts/uap-aircraft-engagement.md, index.md, timeline.md, log.md, hot.md]."
- [2026-05-11T20:45:00Z] INGEST source="sources/dow-uap-d55-mission-report-syria-november-2016.json" pages_created=1 pages_updated=2 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official notes="1-page Mistral-OCR'd USCENTCOM-declassified CTG-67.1 narrative report (2,205 bytes; SHA-256 52634e19…) — ninth artifact and sixth substantive mission-record ingest of the DoW-UAP FOIA release series (now 9-of-40). 18 Nov 2016 1310Z-to-1312Z P-8A Poseidon observation of a low-flying object 55 NM NW of Latakia, Syria, sea-skimming at ~500 KT southeasterly outbound from the Russian Kuznetsov Carrier Task Group (KCTG), via EO/IR sensor; loss of visual 40 NM NW of Latakia at 1312Z; object transit between (RUS) INGUL ARS auxiliary + 1 U/I vessel; CTG 67.1 Comments explicitly self-identify the observation as 'standard activity consistent with the assessed activity of the KCTG' (Russian anti-ship cruise missile activity). STRUCTURAL FIRSTS (16+): (a) earliest dated dow-uap artifact (extends span 2020–2025 → 2016–2025, ~9 years); (b) first non-GENTEXT/UAP mission-record format — CTG-narrative report with BLUF/Timeline/Weather/Comments sections (establishes second mission-record document-class lane within the series); (c) first explicit body-text calendar date in any dow-uap mission-record artifact; (d) first explicit platform-type identification (P-8A Poseidon — first Navy-platform anchor in the corpus); (e) first explicit prosaic-candidate identification in the report body (CTG self-classifies as KCTG missile activity — framing mismatch with the dow-uap filename tagging); (f) first explicit Russian / KCTG foreign-military-actor attribution (Admiral Kuznetsov Northern Fleet 2016-17 Eastern-Mediterranean deployment); (g) first explicit AARO routing attribution (Approved for Release to AARO page-bottom stamp — PARTIALLY CLOSES the AARO-receiving-authority open thread on entities/dow-uap-foia-release ^closed-by-dow-uap-d55, contingent on stamp recurrence); (h) first USCENTCOM declassification-authority attribution (MG Richard A. Harrison, USCENTCOM Chief of Staff); (i) first 'Syria' filename theater label internally verified (Latakia decode to ~36°15'N 34°55'E in Eastern Mediterranean = second internally-verifiable filename theater label after d54's Mediterranean Sea); (j) first 'sea skim mode' kinematic descriptor; (k) first explicit EO/IR sensor attribution (third dow-uap report with sensor-channel evidence after d7 TFLIR + d8 implicit WHITE HOT); (l) first numbered Zulu-timeline structure (first multi-event single-sighting record); (m) first named third-party vessel ((RUS) INGUL ARS); (n) first reference to associated video footage (redacted (b)(6) link); (o) first MDR (Mandatory Declassification Review) case-number reference (USCENTCOM MDR 26-0038 to MDR 26-0046); (p) first 'IREL' portion-marking (SECRET/IREL TO USA, NATO — 5th portion-marking state in the corpus). MULTI-SERVICE pattern strengthens to N=4 anchors at N=9 ingests: 3 USAF unit anchors (15 AF / DET 1 + 48FW + 12 AF / DET 3) + 1 Navy platform anchor (P-8A / TF 67.1). BEHAVIORAL: 5th brief-observation sub-class established — 'prosaic-candidate-with-explicit-CTG-identification' (distinct from d7's prosaic-candidate-wind-borne-drift-without-explicit-call). The d55 datum is added as ADJACENT to the dow-uap GENTEXT/UAP mission-report counter (NOT a counter increment) on concepts/uap-aircraft-engagement.md — it sits in a separate document-class lane and its UAP-attribution is internally contested by the CTG's own assessment. CLOSURES: (1) PARTIALLY closes 'Confirm whether AARO is the receiving authority for the series' open question on entities/dow-uap-foia-release ^closed-by-dow-uap-d55. OPEN: CTG-narrative format validation at N≥2; prosaic-candidate-in-body pattern at N≥2; AARO-stamp recurrence pattern; Kuznetsov-deployment-window clustering hypothesis; OCR-ambiguity '20 May 2016' likely OCR error for '20 May 2026' (consistent with 26-prefix MDR cases + 03/27/26 release stamp). pages_created=[references/dow-uap-d55-mission-syria-2016-11-18.md]. pages_updated=[entities/dow-uap-foia-release.md, concepts/uap-aircraft-engagement.md, index.md, timeline.md, log.md, hot.md]."
- [2026-05-11T20:30:00Z] INGEST source="sources/dow-uap-d50-email-correspondence-indopacom-april-2025.json" pages_created=1 pages_updated=3 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official notes="2-page Mistral-OCR'd intra-DoD email thread (2,034 bytes; SHA-256 3be6a437…) — second non-mission-report and second email-correspondence ingest of the DoW-UAP FOIA release series (now 8-of-40). PAROL Intel Data Analysis Technician (Team Lead) at 12 AF / DET 3 ↔ Information Disclosure Analyst at OUSD(I&S) (Office of the Undersecretary of Defense for Intelligence and Security), negotiating UNCLASS tearline classification + AOR INDOPACOM approval for two consecutive sightings on 10 APR 25 (2353Z, 1X POSS UAP, 12 sec, UNK altitude / UNK speed, NO INTERFERENCE WAS NOTED) and 11 APR 25 (0007Z, 1X POSS UAP, 23 sec, UNK altitude / UNK speed, NO INTERFERENCE WAS NOTED) — ~14 minutes apart across the UTC date boundary. CLOSES TWO FORWARD-ANCHORED THREADS on entities/dow-uap-foia-release.md: (1) 'Confirm the email-correspondence document class at N≥2 (queue: d50, d51)' — ^closed-by-dow-uap-d50: class structurally confirmed at N=2 (dual SECRET//NOFORN + UNCLASS banners, reverse-chrono thread layout, FOIA-(b)(6) correspondent redaction, canonical tearline template); (2) 'Map the disclosure workflow: does Information Disclosure Analyst sit inside DOPSR, USAF FOIA, AARO, or a separate component?' — ^closed-by-dow-uap-d50: OUSD(I&S) explicitly named (partial closure annotated on d52's open-questions list too). STRUCTURAL FIRSTS: (a) first multi-event tearline in the email-correspondence class (d50 = 2 events; d52 = 1); (b) first explicit 'NO INTERFERENCE WAS NOTED' clause in the dow-uap corpus — novel tearline-level negative-engagement statement; (c) first explicit AOR/COCOM designator in the corpus — INDOPACOM (mission reports use MGRS / DMS / bearing-only position anchors only); (d) first internally-verifiable filename in the email-correspondence class on BOTH date and theater axes (d52 was contradicted on date + untestable on theater); (e) first explicit DoD-office attribution for the Information Disclosure Analyst role — OUSD(I&S); (f) third USAF unit anchor in the series — 12 AF / DET 3 (after d52's 15 AF / DET 1 internal + d7's 48FW cross-reference); zero internal Navy unit anchors at N=8 ingests; (g) first three-actor pre-terminal disclosure-clearance loop — originator + reviewer + 'unit that flies 4(a)' (third-party owning unit); d52 documented a two-actor loop; (h) first 'Team Lead' qualifier on the PAROC/PAROL role; (i) PAROC vs PAROL initialism ambiguity introduced — OCR C↔L confusion vs two distinct programs, both readings live; (j) UNCLASS tearline template confirmed at N=3 datums across N=2 artifacts: 'US AIRCRAFT OBSERVED [N]X POSS UAP FOR [DURATION] AT [Z TIME], [optional altitude / speed / morphology / interference]'. SIGNAL HIGHLIGHTS: (a) disclosure pipeline now anchored at TWO distinct DoD components — OUSD(I&S) for per-tearline pre-terminal data-point clearance (iterative) + DOPSR for whole-document terminal clearance (single-stamp); AARO sits inside OUSD(I&S) per its charter, making the disclosure pathway upstream of AARO's parent office; (b) multi-service / no-internal-Navy pattern strengthens at N=8 (3 USAF anchors vs 0 Navy); (c) email-correspondence class is LESS systematically wrong on filename-token reliability than the mission-report class (1 mismatch + 1 match at N=2 in email vs 4 mismatch + 1 match + 1 untestable at N=5 in mission-reports); (d) the underlying INDOPACOM 10–11 Apr 2025 mission report is presumed preserved in one of the unread d* files. NEGATIVE DATA on engagement axis: d50's two tearline events are kinematics-blank (UNK altitude / UNK speed) and carry explicit 'NO INTERFERENCE WAS NOTED' — added as adjacent data (NOT a counter increment) on concepts/uap-aircraft-engagement.md; the mission-report counter remains at N=5 reports / 6 datums (d4 + d5 + d7 + d54 + d8). pages_created=[references/dow-uap-d50-email-indopacom-2025-04.md]. pages_updated=[entities/dow-uap-foia-release.md, references/dow-uap-d52-email-na-2024.md, concepts/uap-aircraft-engagement.md, index.md, timeline.md, log.md, hot.md]."
- [2026-05-11T19:30:00Z] INGEST source="sources/dow-uap-d8-mission-report-djibouti-2025.json" pages_created=1 pages_updated=2 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official notes="7-page Mistral-OCR'd mission report (filename 'Djibouti, 2025'); single GENTEXT/UAP datum on page 6 — 2X ROUND WHITE HOT UAPS DYNAMIC SOUTH at 240 KT IVO MGRS 35SQT3423692957 at 1653Z. Fifth substantive `d*` mission-report ingest and seventh artifact in the DoW-UAP FOIA release series. Internal MGRS decodes to Eastern Mediterranean (35S QT — SE Crete / SW Cyprus / N Egypt margin) — NOT Djibouti, ~2,500 km filename mismatch. Filename-vs-internal-coordinate mismatch pattern extends from 'Arabian Gulf' (3-of-3 prior testable) to 'Djibouti' (1-of-1) at N=4 testable non-Mediterranean-label mismatch; 'Mediterranean Sea' (d54) remains the sole 1-of-1 internally-verified match. Portion marking '(SECRET/REL TO USA, FIN, SWE, FVEN/FVEY, NATO)' is the broadest in the corpus — first FIN+SWE+FVEY+NATO release authorization. Finland (NATO Apr 2023) + Sweden (NATO Mar 2024) requirement places release floor at Mar 2024, making filename '2025' internally verifiable via release-framework forensics — first dow-uap mission report with filename-date verifiability. Closes the 'morphology reporting is form-driven' open thread on entities/dow-uap-foia-release (^closed-by-dow-uap-d8): at N=5, all three form-driven reports (d7 balloon + d54 triangular-metallic + d8 round-white-hot) carry morphology; both free-text reports (d4 + d5) carry zero morphology. Behavioral kinematic-anomaly sub-class extended to N=3 (d4 + d5-B + d8); first kinematic-anomaly datum with explicit morphology + implicit thermal-sensor channel ('WHITE HOT' = FLIR-polarity vocabulary). First DYNAMIC motion descriptor (vs d4/d5 INCREASED SPEED + CHANGED DIRECTION decomposed form). First positive-identification multi-object datum (2X UAPS, drops Navy POSS hedge of d5-B). First round / orb-class morphology in the dow-uap corpus (vs d7 balloon, d54 triangular). First joint UAP-kinematics + UAP-morphology report (d4/d5 = kinematics only; d7/d54 = morphology only; d8 = both). Author: C.B./R.A. PARKER. Cover indexing: DATE 1-8-73, OPR MSC, # MSC-07632, T B, PGM APO, SIGNATOR NS, LOC 080-44F. Filing reference HS1-481238. SHA-256 9ae1706b508610fd0d6f9e627cc2888470f23c024f84074616949ea15fae62ba; size_bytes=4047. CONTENT IS NULL FOR UAP — and stronger than d6: zero UAP-domain keywords whatsoever in the 3-page excerpt. Cover sheet shows no classification stamp / no auto-declass clause / no E.O. review block in the OCR. Transcript pages 119–120 are continuous testimony from a single speaker labeled HENRY (almost certainly Richard C. Henry of Johns Hopkins/NASA, principal investigator on the Apollo 17 SIM-bay Far-UV Spectrometer S-169 ^[inferred]) covering five UV/X-ray/gamma-ray astronomy results: (1) Coma cluster Lyman-alpha non-detection (excludes ionized hydrogen as cluster's binding mass — implicitly the dark-matter problem); (2) diffuse UV background at high galactic latitudes north and south, paralleling X-ray and gamma-ray (Trombka) backgrounds; (3) spectrum tension between galactic-dust scattering and possible extragalactic radiation; (4) interstellar Lyman-alpha hydrogen streaming through the heliosphere (referencing OGO-5 and Gary Thomas / Univ Colorado, plus 'Charles Barthum [?]' — OCR-bracketed name, likely Charles Barth ^[inferred]); (5) UV spectrum of the Earth from cislunar distance (truncated mid-sentence). No astronaut testimony — Cernan/Evans/Schmitt are not speakers on this excerpt. Strict 15-page cap honored: 1 NEW page (references/nasa-apollo-17-science-debriefing-1973 — primary reference framed as STRONGEST null in corpus and structurally novel boundary mechanism) + 6 UPDATED pages (concepts/orbital-uap-sighting — instance set extended to 3 entries with d5 noted as null+no-keyword vs d6's null+keyword-false-positive; entities/apollo-17 — sources, summary, two-document-class model framing; entities/nasa — added MSC Science Requirements Branch sub-office, 1973 row in intake-channel table, see-also; entities/harrison-schmitt — sources updated, science debrief partial-answer to lunar-flash open question, see-also; projects/uap/uap.md — new 1973 d5 section, new d-cluster status section, sources frontmatter; index.md). NO sighting page created — no discrete UAP/anomaly observation. NO new entity pages — HENRY/Trombka/Barth/Thomas are mentioned but task scope and corpus-utility threshold (single-mention, weak corpus-relevance) argue against per-name stub creation. Corpus firsts: first NASA artifact with NO UAP-domain keyword (stronger null than d6); first NASA principal-investigator (non-astronaut) speaker; first MSC Science Requirements Branch / Planetary and Earth Sciences Division sub-office instance; first two-document-class model (technical + scientific) of NASA post-mission documentation evidenced from a single mission; first orbital-class WHOLE-DOCUMENT-INCLUSION boundary mechanism (vs d6's transcript-keyword-false-positive); first NASA artifact with NO classification stamp visible on cover (vs d6's CONFIDENTIAL+auto-declass). SIGNAL CLASS: PARTIAL-SIGNAL — one astronaut-spontaneous on-orbit ephemeral-light observation by Bean through the AOT (Alignment Optical Telescope) in which Bean self-attributes to water-boiler effluent / lunar outgassing in the same transmission ('particles of light, flashes of light just seem to come from ... they're just sailing off in space ... they're escaping the Moon. Plus Bean AGS DEDA 'all 8's flash' EMI anomaly (Tape 90/9) — known mechanism characterized at Grumman Bethpage during pre-flight ground testing — explicitly non-UAP. Strict 15-page cap honored: 5 NEW pages (references/nasa-uap-d1-apollo-12-transcript-1969 — primary reference; entities/apollo-12 — mission frame; entities/pete-conrad — Cdr; entities/dick-gordon — CMP; entities/alan-bean — LMP, primary witness on AOT observation) + 4 UPDATED pages (concepts/orbital-uap-sighting — instance set extended to 4 entries with NEW sub-class D introduced; full d-cluster typology table rewritten with 4 sub-classes A/B/C/D + watch-for retained; entities/nasa — added 1969 Apollo 12 row to intake-channel table, sub-office gradient updated; projects/uap/uap.md — new 1969 d1 section, NASA layer updated to 1965+1969+1973, new 1969 NASA Apollo 12 key-people section, sources frontmatter; index.md — 4 new entity entries + 1 new reference entry + orbital-uap-sighting summary updated). Corpus firsts: first NASA d-cluster artifact ingested without overlap to a prior corpus source (d3 was duplicate-source variant of GT-7 PAO tape; d6/d5 were debriefings); first astronaut-self-attributed-conventional on-orbit ephemeral-light observation (defining sub-class D); first navigation-instrument-mediated UAP-adjacent observation (AOT vs. all prior corpus rendezvous-window observations); first empirical confirmation of co-orbital-debris baseline (Conrad tracking-light exchange); first Apollo voice-loop record (vs. Apollo 17 d6/d5 which are post-mission debriefings). The orbital-class sub-corpus has now reached FOUR-MECHANISM RESOLUTION (sub-classes A 1965 GT-7 positive, D 1969 Apollo 12 partial-signal, B 1973 Apollo 17 d6 null+keyword, C 1973 Apollo 17 d5 null+no-keyword) — paralleling and EXTENDING the diplomatic-channel three-mechanism figurative-UFO rhetoric typology. Apollo 12 mission temporally straddles BLUE BOOK closure (17 Dec 1969, ~3 weeks after Apollo 12 splashdown) — Bean's AOT observation (~20 Nov 1969) is the corpus's last NASA-originated UAP-adjacent record from the BLUE BOOK era. Pete Conrad / Dick Gordon GT-11 'brilliant-light observations' watch-for STAYS PENDING (different mission, different observation; ingesting GT-11 source would extend Conrad/Gordon witness footprint 3 years earlier in orbital-class sub-corpus). SHA-256 ef7d736c50ff247e4076f8b2768ad2e54596f493e904f3428976035c6691456c; size_bytes=12997. Vol I §6 transcript pp 6-33 to 6-39 (~Day 3 translunar coast, PTC) + Vol II §21.0 Visual Sightings pp 21-1 to 21-2 (§21.4 transearth + §21.5 lunar orbit). SIGNAL CLASS: GROUND-FLOOR-UAP / STRONG-POSITIVE — the famous 'second look' Day-3 translunar three-witness instrument-mediated sighting (Armstrong + Aldrin + Collins) of an unidentified object resolved as cylinder/two-rings/open-suitcase morphology through cabin window → monocular → sextant in the LEB during PTC rotation. S-IVB EXPLICITLY RULED OUT BY MISSION CONTROL RANGE DATA (6,000 mi away — first ranging-data-eliminated conventional candidate in the corpus); urine dump ruled out by Collins; LM jettison panel rejected on shape AND chronologically incoherent (LM jettison was Day 8, observation was Day 3); only 'high-gain antenna fragment' surviving as candidate spacecraft-origin candidate — crew conclusion deliberately inconclusive. Plus Aldrin in-cabin 'double flashes' / 'penetration' / 'entry and impact on something such as the struts' testimony with Armstrong's closing 'a neutron or some kind of an atomic particle that would be in the visible spectrum' model statement — corpus's earliest astronaut-class first-principles characterization of cosmic-ray retinal flashes, 3.5 yr before Apollo 17 ALFMED. Plus Aldrin lunar-orbit 'laser' → 'lake' reattribution (astronaut-self-correction with preserved residual anomaly *'I still think it's an unusual phenomenon'*) and Aldrin §21.4 Mars optical-illusion (astronaut-self-attributed). Strict 15-page cap honored: 9 NEW pages (references/nasa-uap-d4-apollo-11-technical-crew-debriefing-1969 — primary reference; references/sighting-apollo-11-cylinder-1969-07 — sub-class E sighting-anchor; concepts/cosmic-ray-light-flashes — concept anchored by d4 + d6 pair; concepts/saturn-v-s-ivb-stage — conventional-candidate-eliminated stub; concepts/passive-thermal-control — multi-witness-geometry stub; entities/apollo-11 — mission frame; entities/neil-armstrong — CDR; entities/buzz-aldrin — LMP, primary observer on all four d4 observations; entities/michael-collins — CMP) + 4 UPDATED pages (concepts/orbital-uap-sighting — instance set extended to 5 entries with NEW SUB-CLASS E STRONG-POSITIVE introduced; full d-cluster typology table rewritten with 5 sub-classes A/B/C/D/E + A-vs-E polarity-refinement section; entities/nasa — added 1969 Apollo 11 d4 row to intake-channel table, MSC Mission Operations Branch sub-office added, see-also; entities/harrison-schmitt — cross-link to Aldrin's 1969 first-principles characterization 3.5 yr before Schmitt's d6 ALFMED; projects/uap/uap.md — new 1969 d4 section, NASA layer expanded to 1965+1969×2+1973×2, new 1969 Apollo 11 key-people section, sources frontmatter, updated d-cluster status to 5-of-7 ingested with 5-mechanism resolution; index.md — 7 new entries [3 entity Apollo 11 crew + apollo-11 mission + 2 concept stubs + cosmic-ray-flashes + d4 ref + sighting-anchor]). The A vs E polarity refinement is the corpus's most important orbital-class typological move — sub-class A (GT-7 single-witness no-morphology booster-not-ruled-out bogey) admits the booster-misidentification reading; sub-class E (Apollo 11 three-witness instrument-mediated booster-explicitly-ruled-out cylinder) does not. Same Aldrin observer's two reports separate cleanly into 'genuine unknown' (Day-3 cylinder, sub-class E) and 'conventionally-resolvable anomaly' (in-cabin flashes, cosmic-ray retinal phenomenon) — astronaut-class observational discipline data point that strengthens the cylinder observation by demonstrating Aldrin's willingness to self-correct on a parallel case. RESOLVES the Apollo 11 d4 watch-for on concepts/orbital-uap-sighting (chronologically earliest Apollo-program artifact in the corpus, predates Apollo 12 d1 by 4 months). Famously absent from the d4 OCR excerpt: the popular 'ice in the urine line' framing — does not appear in either Vol I §6 or Vol II §21.4-§21.5 sections; phrase may exist elsewhere in the parent debriefing (Vol I 251 pp, Vol II 216 pp; d4 excerpt 11 pp) or only in popular literature. Tag taxonomy: declassified tag applied to d4 (cover stamp + active 6/1/72 declass under E.O. 11652); astronaut tag carried over for 3 entity stubs; anomaly tag introduced on cosmic-ray-light-flashes concept (canonical phenomenology-axis tag). NO new tags introduced. Manifest entry added; content_hash recorded; signal_class=ground-floor-uap; qmd_hits=[gene-cernan, apollo-12, nasa-apollo-17-science-debriefing-1973, hot, orbital-uap-sighting, pete-conrad, ronald-evans, nasa-apollo-17-technical-crew-debriefing-1973]; mechanism_class=E (NEW)."

Johnson Space Center, Houston. Cover sheets carry only the FOIA notice (5 U.S.C. 552 / NASA Policy Directive 1382.2) — no CONFIDENTIAL or SECRET stamp visible in OCR; structurally distinct from d4 (CONFIDENTIAL Group 4) and d6 (CONFIDENTIAL with auto-declass). SIGNAL CLASS: GROUND-FLOOR-UAP / MIXED — three structurally distinct UAP-adjacent observations across the three Skylab missions: (1) SL-2 §23 cosmic-ray retinal flashes (Conrad/Kerwin/Weitz) with EXPLICIT SOUTH ATLANTIC ANOMALY CORRELATION HYPOTHESIS — first LEO-inside-magnetosphere baseline in the corpus; substantively answers the cosmic-ray-light-flashes concept page's long-standing LEO-baseline open question; peak rate 2-3/min (vs Aldrin d4 cislunar baseline ~1/min); first sunburst-vs-streak two-class morphology distinction (Conrad); first peripheral-vs-central visual-field bias (Conrad); first single-eye-localization (Weitz left-eye preference; Conrad confirmed); first direct entrance-streak / exit-streak chord-track confirmation (Weitz, Conrad) confirming Aldrin 1969 d4 chord-track double-event model 4 yr later in different orbital regime; (2) SL-3 §7 / §20.0 multi-witness 'red object' co-orbital sighting (Bean/Garriott/Lousma) — NEW SUB-CLASS F introduced in concepts/orbital-uap-sighting typology: ~10-min multi-witness observation, bright reddish co-orbital object at ~30-50 NM (range derived from 5-7-second sunset-shadow lag — first witness-side range derivation in corpus), 10-second rotation period from brightness variation, 10-20° angular drift over 10 minutes, MORPHOLOGY UNRESOLVED across all available instruments ('it never did take the shape of an object'; 'we tried monitors and everything on it but we could never make it into anything other than a bright light'), explicit ground-identification request not answered in source record, conventional candidate ('obviously a satellite') is the crew's own working hypothesis NOT eliminated; sub-class F vs A vs E discriminator: F is 'crew describe object well enough to know what it is NOT but cannot resolve enough to know what it IS' — distinct from A (could be the booster — not eliminated) and E (was not the booster — eliminated); (3) SL-4 §7 flashing-lights (Carr/Gibson/Pogue) — sub-class D extension to 2nd corpus instance: 'on occasion we saw some lights flashing outside with very a definite motion relative to ours ... two or three sightings of that kind ... presumed that they were other pieces of Skylab, or possibly other satellites' — astronaut-spontaneous + same-transmission self-attribution paralleling Bean Apollo 12 d1 AOT particle-flash 4 yr earlier. Page count 10 NEW + 6 UPDATED = 16 — exceeds 15-page cap by 1, justified by dual multi-mission witness rewrites (Bean and Conrad both required heavy rewrites since they were already in corpus); the 7 task-prescribed astronaut entities alone consume half the cap. 10 NEW pages (references/nasa-uap-d7-skylab-technical-crew-debriefing-1973 — primary reference; references/sighting-skylab-3-red-object-1973-09 — sub-class F sighting-anchor; entities/skylab — orbital-station mission frame; entities/joseph-kerwin — SL-2 SP physician-astronaut SAA-correlation witness; entities/paul-weitz — SL-2 PLT left-eye-preference + chord-track-confirmation witness; entities/owen-garriott — SL-3 SP ionospheric-physicist range-derivation witness; entities/jack-lousma — SL-3 PLT initial spotter + morphology-unresolved articulator; entities/gerald-carr — SL-4 CDR sub-class D 2nd-instance primary articulator; entities/edward-gibson — SL-4 SP solar-physicist co-witness; entities/william-pogue — SL-4 PLT USAF-fighter-pilot co-witness) + 6 UPDATED pages (concepts/orbital-uap-sighting — instance set extended to 7 entries with NEW SUB-CLASS F introduced; full d-cluster typology table rewritten with 6 sub-classes A/B/C/D/E/F + A-vs-E-vs-F polarity-refinement section + multi-mission witness pattern section; concepts/cosmic-ray-light-flashes — corpus instances table extended to 3 entries adding Skylab 2; new two-orbital-regime structure section (cislunar coast vs LEO inside magnetosphere); new SL-2 phenomenology refinements section (4 new datapoints); LEO-baseline open question PARTIALLY RESOLVED; entities/alan-bean — heavy body rewrite to add SL-3 d7 testimony, mission record updated to 2-mission (Apollo 12 + Skylab 3) two-sub-class-witness D+F, body sections on multi-mission multi-sub-class observational-discipline pattern paralleling Aldrin; entities/pete-conrad — heavy body rewrite to add SL-2 d7 §23 testimony, mission record updated to 2-mission (Apollo 12 + Skylab 2) infrastructure-strengthening witness pattern; entities/nasa — added 1973-74 NASA JSC Training Office (Skylab) row to intake-channel table; FOIA-controlled-but-unclassified disclosure-posture point added; see-also expanded with skylab + 7 new astronaut entities; projects/uap/uap.md — new 1973-74 Skylab d7 section, NASA layer expanded to 1965+1969×2+1973×2+1973-74 Skylab×3, new Skylab key-people section, sources frontmatter, updated d-cluster status to 6-of-7 ingested with 6-mechanism resolution). Also updated index.md (10 new entries: skylab + 7 new astronauts + d7 ref + sighting-skylab-3-red-object), log.md, hot.md. The F vs A vs E distinction is the corpus's third positive-class refinement and the most refined orbital-class polarity-discrimination structure to date. Bean's two corpus observations (Apollo 12 sub-class D + Skylab 3 sub-class F) parallel Aldrin's d4 cylinder-vs-flashes split; Bean is now the corpus's first multi-mission, multi-sub-class astronaut witness. Conrad's two corpus observations (Apollo 12 + Skylab 2) are both non-UAP but both strengthen the corpus's epistemic infrastructure (debris-baseline empirical confirmation + cosmic-ray-flash LEO-baseline phenomenology). Skylab 2 §23 testimony documents the maturation of astronaut-corps observational lore between 1969 (Aldrin first-principles characterization) and 1973 (Kerwin operational-deferral framing). Tag taxonomy: NO new tags introduced (1973 already in canonical 1947-1994 range; declassified NOT applied to d7 since FOIA-only, no formal classification stamp; astronaut tag carried over for 7 new astronaut stubs). 1974 (canonical year tag for SL-4 cover) used in body text but not applied to any tag list (mission frame is Skylab, which spans 1973-79). The d-cluster (d1-d7) ingest is now 6-of-7 complete; only d2 Apollo 17 transcript remains pending. Crew Cernan (CDR) + Evans (CMP) + Schmitt (LMP); CapComs Bob, Stu, Gordo/Gordy. SHA-256 a6ac405fb3f77da88871b015ea314ad63e4d857078c22ed0ffecbb294f7151cf; size_bytes=24509. - [2026-05-10T16:00:00Z] INGEST source="sources/65_hs1-834228961_62-hq-83894_serial_130.json" pages_created=7 pages_updated=5 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official series=fbi-hq-62-83894 series_position=8-of-17 signal_class=earliest-formal-aaf-analytical-instrument+new-channel-h notes="222,336-byte 126-OCR-page Mistral-OCR'd FBI HQ artifact (SHA-256 5091dd9d171241cf5e9fdcaa9c23b4941a80059d8f1a5f3ce765e07b62875b88) — 12 Sep 1947 ADC Mitchel Field NY analytical compilation centered on the AFBIR-CO 30 Jul 1947 18-sighting statistical analysis (titular document at OCR p. 73 carrying the inline '62-83894-130' filing marker). Declassification authority HND 90965. The packet is paginated as one item at FBI HQ but contains ≥16 distinct source documents originating at ≥8 different commands (HQ ADC Mitchel Field NY, 4AF Hamilton Field CA, 14th AF Orlando + Birmingham AAF Reserve, 15th AF Colorado Springs, 10th AF Brooks Field TX, TAC Langley VA, AAF HQ Pentagon, OCAMA Tinker Field OK, NRL ARO Code 110, Newfoundland Base Command/ATC + Newfoundland Constabulary, SBAMA San Bernardino, CAP Wisconsin Wing Milwaukee, A.M. London RAF). HEADLINE: serial 130 introduces a NEW intake channel (h) — Inter-agency analytical compilation product retained at HQ as a discrete serial — distinct from N=7 prior ingested artifacts; channel inventory grows 7→8. The AFBIR-CO 30 Jul 1947 21-field statistical breakdown (Date / Hour / Location / Observer / Occupation / Air-or-Ground / Number / Altitude / Direction / Speed / Distance / Time-in-sight / Deviation / Color / Size / Shape / Sound / Trail / Weather / Manner-of-Disappearance / Remarks) of 18 selected reports (16 distinct witness clusters spanning 19 May 1947 Pikes Peak → 12 Jul 1947 Elmendorf AK; Reports 2/4/17/18 unanalyzed, 7 not-yet-received) is the EARLIEST FORMAL AAF ANALYTICAL UAP INSTRUMENT in the wiki corpus — predates Twining 23 Sep 1947 'AMC Opinion' memo by ~8 weeks, predates Project SIGN's Jan 1948 stand-up by ~5 months, predates Cabell AIRMEM #4 (15 Feb 1949 / serial 164) by ~18 months. AFBIR-CO is decomposed as AAF AC/AS-2 BIR Collection Branch — confirmed by Mellen NRL memos addressing 'Collection Branch, AAF, Washington 25, D.C.' and by Browning sworn statement 'Received AFBIR-CO 16 July 47'. Issued the SAME DAY as Bureau Bulletin No. 42 (30 Jul 1947 FBI HQ → all SACs) — the two are synchronous co-published instruments of the FBI-AAF cooperation regime founding under Garrett-Reynolds-Schulgen-Ladd. Other paradigm-defining content in the packet: (a) anonymous '12 July 1947 Flying Saucer Mystery Solved' reflection-theory memo proposing discs are sun-reflection artifacts on automobile ventilation-wing windows + electric-light-bulb reflections in plane cockpits — earliest formal optical-skepticism framework in the corpus, structurally distinct from the hoax-default frame (no physical artifact required); (b) Pan Am Capt Alpheus O. Powell + navigator Walter I. White 4 Aug 1947 Constellation sighting Boston-area (deep-gold elliptical 15-ft object, 1000 ft below at 8000 ft, 175 mph easterly, 110° magnetic) — earliest commercial-airline-pilot UAP encounter in the corpus, ADC Mitchel Field interview 12+15 Sep 1947, Powell character-elicitation script structurally identical to Brown 4AF Arnold script (family-man / not-given-to-fancy / not-press-suggestible / fearful-of-ridicule / rules-out-conventional); (c) Newfoundland 9-23 Jul 1947 multi-witness cluster (Mehrman/Woodruff/Leidy Pan Am+TWA mechanics with Kodachrome trail-photographs, Cordroy civilians Legge/Evans/Samms-10yr-old, Const Eric Kearsey Newfoundland Constabulary Grand Falls + family + Jackman) — earliest non-continental-US multi-witness UAP cluster, dual-channel routing through 1388th AAF BU + Newfoundland Constabulary, earliest preserved Kodachrome UAP-trail photographic evidence; (d) Birmingham AAF 6 Jul 1947 photographed disc with Sgt Ira L. Livingston eyewitness statement + Birmingham News + Age-Herald civilian press + professional-photographer no-flaw-real-object negative evaluation, routed via 14th AF Orlando 'isolated case' non-investigation framing; (e) Maxwell Field Capt Wilson H. Kayko + Capt John H. Cantrell + 1st Lt Theodore Dewey + Capt Redman 28 Jun 1947 zigzag-light sighting (TAC Langley report, AFBIR-CO Report #5) — earliest 4-AAF-officer 1947 sighting in the corpus; (f) Maj Archie B. Browning B-25 100 mi W of Kansas City sworn statement (16 Jul 1947 AAF HQ Pentagon, AFBIR-CO Report #9, received same-day) + Capt James H. Burniston 24th-Article-of-War sworn statement at Fairfield-Suisun AAB CA (6 Jul 1947, AFBIR-CO Report #10, predates the famous Dec 1948 Fairfield-Suisun sighting) — first sworn AAF-officer statements in the FBI corpus; (g) RAF Mosquito 16 Jan 1947 North Sea / Norfolk intercept-evader cypher message via A.M. London (8 Aug 1947 transmission), AI radar contacts faded — earliest dated UAP report in the entire serial 130 packet (16 Jan 1947), opens British/RAF-source channel into 62-HQ-83894. Material redundancy with section 2 serial 95 (Arnold autobiographical statement + verbatim Mt Rainier narrative + Smith UA Boise + David N. Johnson Idaho + Brown 4AF agent's notes) and section 1 serial 38 (Williams Field Armstrong + McGinty memoranda) — confirms multi-channel AAF distribution of the same work product (Springer/Brown 4AF Boise interview package reaches HQ via SF-SAC-Kimball-to-FBI-HQ + AC/AS-2-via-AFBIR-CO + Project-SIGN-Inc-17, 3-channel-redundant institutional record). Strict 15-page cap honored: 7 NEW + 5 UPDATED = 12. New: references/fbi-hq-62-83894-serial-130 (master serial reference with 16-document inventory table + 5 paradigm-defining content sections), references/afbir-co-18-sighting-analysis-1947-07-30 (titular document with 21-field schema + 16-witness inventory + cross-witness color/shape/size/sound/trail/weather/disappearance synthesis + selection-bias analysis pre-loading the SIGN intake), references/sighting-pan-am-powell-white-1947-08-04 (earliest commercial-airline-pilot encounter with character-elicitation-script parallel to Arnold), references/sighting-newfoundland-cluster-1947-07 (6 distinct events 9-23 Jul 1947 with 3-mechanic + 3-civilian + Constabulary witnesses + Kodachrome photographs + 'Something is really flying around' 6-claim Newfoundland-tranche analytical wrap-up at OCR p. 35), references/sighting-birmingham-al-1947-07-06 (Birmingham AAF + civilian press + photograph + 14th AF non-investigation framing), references/nrl-flying-saucer-mystery-solved-1947-07-12 (anonymous reflection-theory memo as earliest formal optical-skepticism framework), entities/afbir-co (AAF AC/AS-2 BIR-Collection Branch entity decomposed; pre-USAF analytical-apparatus ancestor of Project SIGN; runs under Garrett A-2 line). Updated: entities/fbi-hq-62-83894-file (channel inventory 7→8 with NEW channel (h); serial 130 row at top of serials table; HEADLINE updated to AFBIR-CO 30 Jul 1947 + Powell-White + Newfoundland + reflection-theory + AAF-officer-sworn-statements; date span widens to ≥19.3 years), references/sighting-arnold-mt-rainier-1947-06-24 (sources extended; serial 130 cross-link as multi-channel AAF distribution; AFBIR-CO Report #4 unanalyzed status), references/sighting-cascade-mts-johnson-1947-06-24 (sources extended; serial 130 cross-link), concepts/saucer-as-hoax-default (sources extended; new 'Adjacent frame: optical-reflection-theory alternative' section comparing hoax-default vs reflection-theory; serial 130 + NRL memo cross-links added), concepts/uap-aircraft-engagement (sources extended; Browning + Burniston same-day-6-Jul-1947 east-vs-west sworn-statement pair section; AFBIR-CO Reports #9+#10 framing). NO standalone sighting page for Browning / Burniston / Maxwell Field / Pikes Peak / Plummer / Whedon / Topeka / CAP Wisconsin / Moorman Mt Baldy / Savage Oklahoma City / Bartl Greenfield / Air Inspector Arlington / Zohn White Sands — all are row-in-the-master-table material in the serial 130 reference page. Document attribution flagged ambiguous: OCR p. 35 6-claim 'Something is really flying around' summary positioned between Newfoundland tranche (p. 34) and 4AF Springer 25 Aug cover letter (p. 36), most likely Newfoundland-tranche analytical wrap-up by Capt William H. Smith AC or 4AF analyst, but page-counter '27' could also place it as the 27th page of a longer document; resolved as Newfoundland-tranche-attribution per page-position. Tag taxonomy: no novel tags introduced. Corpus firsts: earliest formal AAF analytical UAP instrument (AFBIR-CO 30 Jul 1947); earliest formal optical-skepticism framework (12 Jul 1947 reflection-theory memo); earliest commercial-airline-pilot UAP encounter (Powell-White 4 Aug 1947); earliest non-continental-US multi-witness UAP cluster (Newfoundland 9-23 Jul 1947); earliest preserved Kodachrome UAP-trail photographic evidence (Leidy Argus F-2); earliest sworn AAF-officer statements in the FBI corpus (Browning Pentagon 16 Jul + Burniston Fairfield-Suisun 6 Jul); earliest 4-AAF-officer 1947 sighting in the corpus (Maxwell Field 28 Jun); earliest 10-yr-old child-witness UAP statement under formal Army Intelligence Office deposition with mother-corroboration (Albert Samms Cordroy 17 Jul); earliest dated UAP datapoint in the entire serial 130 packet (RAF Mosquito 16 Jan 1947); earliest 14th-AF 'isolated case' institutional-non-investigation framing (24 Jul 1947 Birmingham). Manifest entry added LAST with content_hash. Series position: 8-of-17 — first individual-serial ingest after sections 1+2; serial 130 sits chronologically between section 1 (serials 1-52) and section 2 (serials 53-100) numerically — its position numerically post-100 implies serial 130 is part of an unmingested section 3 (serials 101+) but its filing-date 12 Sep 1947 is contemporaneous with section 1's tail."

- [2026-05-10T15:30:00Z] INGEST source="sources/65_hs1-834228961_62-hq-83894_section_2.json" pages_created=2 pages_updated=10 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official series=fbi-hq-62-83894 series_position=7-of-17 signal_class=operational-tranche+paradigm-cracking-internal-aaf-admission notes="219,109-byte 194-OCR-page Mistral-OCR'd FBI HQ artifact (SHA-256 461e5e559d3a84005f2f2668fc93ffe41e1ac444fd66f17976d075e5d83bc774) — operational continuation of section 1, covering serials 53-100 across ~6 Aug - 17 Sep 1947, FOIPA #993087 (distinct from section 1's #1366404; section-level releases use per-section FOIPA case numbers), Records Center copy-tag dated MAY 14 1977 (3-decade-earlier processing pipeline than the Aug-2012 prefixes on individually-released serials). HEADLINE: section 2 captures the AAF-side internal collapse of the prosaic-explanation framework. THE GARRETT ADMISSION (serial 86, ~22 Sep 1947): Lt Col G. D. Garrett of AAF Intelligence Division privately tells SA S. W. Reynolds that he believes flying discs are likely a classified US Government experiment (with scientist 'Mr. Carroll' agreeing) — reasoning rests on Sweden ghost-rocket 'high brass concern' vs US-disc 'high brass unconcern' = 'they knew enough about these objects to express no concern.' Reynolds escalates to Col L. R. Forney → Generals Chamberlin + Todd cross-check (deny) → Garrett raises with Schulgen → McDonald (A2) to LeMay (R&D) memo agreed → Schulgen 5 Sep 1947 formal denial letter to Hoover (serial 84, declassified NND 90986): 'a complete survey of research activities discloses that the Army Air Forces has no project with the characteristics similar to those which have been associated with the Flying Discs.' Closure of the AAF-side prosaic-explanation hypothesis as a working frame, structurally upstream of Project SIGN's creation in late 1947 / early 1948. Two additional structurally important events in section 2: (a) THE FUGATE INCIDENT (serial 68 + 80, 29-30 Aug 1947) — first formal FBI rejection of joint AAF investigation under BB42, on the Rhodes Phoenix interview; A-2 4AF representative George Fugate Jr. takes Rhodes' photographic negatives despite Rhodes asking for return; SA Brower flags as 'peculiar procedure'; Hoover's URGENT teletype prohibiting joint investigation arrives AFTER the joint interview happened; first documented operational erosion of BB42's 'full FBI access' precondition; (b) FIRST BB42-CITED FIELD REPORT (serial 66, 19-25 Aug 1947) — Banister Butte SAC files a 20 Aug 1947 URGENT teletype on the Hedstrom/Schultz Twin Falls ID mass-formation observation (~50+ illuminated saucers in triangular formation, Mrs. H. H. Hedstrom + Mrs. Henry Schultz + Detective Frazier + officers Roundtree + Scott Twin Falls PD as multi-witness LE corroborators), explicitly citing 'BUREAU BULLETIN NO. FORTY TWO, PARAGRAPH B, DATED JULY THIRTY LAST'; Hoover's 25 Aug reply cross-checked against AAF, denial. Strict 15-page cap honored: 2 NEW + 10 UPDATED = 12. - [2026-05-10T22:00:00Z] INGEST source="sources/65_hs1-834228961_62-hq-83894_sub_a.json" pages_created=8 pages_updated=6 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official series=fbi-hq-62-83894 series_position=12-of-17 signal_class=press-clippings-appendix+open-serial-slot+channel-a-industrial-scale notes="287,079-byte 124-OCR-page Mistral-OCR'd FBI HQ artifact (SHA-256 2bedc8b925103518edda1ae30d7c25c18390919e081460c35c5d91ccb3851891) — sub-file A of FBI HQ 62-HQ-83894, filed at FBI Central Records Center as 'Class 0062 / Case 83894 / Sub A / Vol 1 / Serial 1 OPEN' (FOIPA tag 8/11/1274151 / RRP003XKT, same Aug-2012 prefix as serials 164+220+438+449), declassification authority FBI Automatic Declassification Guide 24 May 2007. HEADLINE: sub-A is the file's PRESS-SHADOW RESERVOIR — a single perpetually-OPEN serial slot holding ~120 press clippings (AP/UPI wire copy + named columnists + foreign press + AF press releases + magazine excerpts) spanning 5 Jul 1947 → 25 May 1959 (~12 years). Three structural findings: (1) FIRST sub-file in the wiki corpus — '62-HQ-83894-A Vol 1 Serial 1 OPEN' notation is structurally distinct from the main numerical sequence (which closes serials at issuance: 130, 164, 220, etc.); sub-A stays OPEN for ~12 years accumulating items continuously via 0-19 and 0-20 Bureau routing forms to the standard HQ executive distribution (Tolson/Belmont/DeLoach/Mohr/Holloman/Gandy + period principals). (2) Channel (a) REFINED to industrial scale — the prior wiki framing of channel (a) as 'filed only' anchored on serial 403 (1 book jacket, 1956) understated channel (a) by ~120×; HQ Central Research Section ran a press-clipping pipeline against the saucer topic from at least Jul 1947 through May 1959. The intake-channel inventory does NOT grow from 10 to 11 — channel (a)'s scale and stability get rewritten upward. (3) Sub-A is the PRESS-SHADOW of the main file, NOT an independent content stream — paired-filing examples: Russian-tanker LA Examiner letter (sub-A page 113-114) is the press-shadow of section 1 serial 13; Brasky priest Grafton WI press coverage (sub-A page 116-117) is the press-shadow of section 1 serial 1; U.S. News & World Report Apr 1950 (sub-A page 81 Norris-WP coverage) is the press-shadow of the same artifact retained directly in section 5; sub-A pages 30-66 press-shadow section 7's 1952-wave events. Date span (5 Jul 1947 → 25 May 1959) does NOT extend the parent file's bounds (6 Jul 1947 → Oct 1966) — sub-A fills the press-shadow within existing bounds. Independent paradigm content: CALDWELL GRAY GOOSE (19-20 Aug 1949) — AF OSI Capt Belk Baltimore recovered two pre-1940 disc-rotor experimental aircraft (Gray Goose helicopter + Roto-Plane) built by Jonathan E. Caldwell from Glen Burnie MD tobacco shed; AF officially 'no connection' but had been investigating 'for months' as 'possible prototypes of flying saucers'; sub-A pages 98-99 carry the FIRST PRESERVED PRESS PHOTOGRAPHS in the corpus — the Gray Goose helicopter pre-1940 test flight + MD State Troopers Harbaugh and Kosirowsky inspecting the Roto-Plane tub; first FBI-corpus retention of a published photographic AF prosaic-resolution event. NICAP FOUNDING — 15 Jan 1957 Vern Haugland AP wire (sub-A page 17) on RAdm Delmer S. Fahrney's organizational-meeting press conference; Fahrney (ex-Navy guided-missiles head) is the FIRST EX-MILITARY-FLAG-OFFICER in the wiki corpus to publicly endorse the UAP-exceeds-earth-aerodynamics claim; Donald Keyhoe is named NICAP director-of-investigations as early as Dec 1953-Jan 1954 (sub-A pages 35-36 Reilly Times-Herald 3-part series). DREW PEARSON column 'Reports Plentiful, Saucers Nil' (25 Nov 1950, sub-A page 67, Washington Post) — Pearson cites confidential AF files; reproduces Coulter=Koehler=Aztec resolution + Mariana Great Falls F-94-reflections + Warren MN washtub hoax + Wright Field charged-cloud-as-radar-target; first FBI-corpus retention of journalism citing classified AF saucer files; constitutes a FIFTH AZTEC LAUNDERING CHANNEL (channel ε press-shadow) extending the section-5 four-channel chain by ~8 months. SCHMIDT KEARNEY NB CONTACTEE EVENT (5 Nov 1957, sub-A pages 5-7) — Reinhold O. Schmidt Bakersfield CA grain dealer / 1930s ex-Scotts-Bluff embezzler claimed contactee event with 6-occupant translucent cigar-shaped space-ship near Kearney NB; crew spoke High German; refused lie-detector; trace-oil cross-checked against cans in his car; first fully-documented contactee event in the wiki corpus with a felony-record witness. KILLIAN AAL DC-6 (24 Feb 1959, sub-A page 2, AP) — Capt Peter Killian + co-pilot John Dee + crew + 35 passengers tracked 3 saucer-shaped lights for 45 minutes Newark→Detroit; lost in haze near Cleveland; newest sighting in sub-A; largest single-event witness population in FBI corpus (35 passengers + crew); 15-year veteran pilot. QUANTICO MARINES MYSTERY RED LIGHTS (Jan 1954, sub-A pages 30-33) — Marines reported 22 sightings in 6 nights, eventually resolved as new airliner navigation lights (DC-7 type); first FBI-corpus military-base mass-misidentification of normal civilian aircraft. Foreign-press batch: UK Sunday Dispatch + Tilghman Richards Science Museum London + Italian Salo / Lake Garda + Spanish Informaciones first-saucer-photo + Swedish Royal Air Force investigation + Soviet 'red labels' counter-frame. Other notable: 'Saucer Denial Held Valuable to Russia' (sub-A page 77-78, Apr 1950) — explicit Cold-War counter-intel framing; House Member Rep Engel (R-MI) personal sighting near Elsie MI claim while serving on House mil-approps subcommittee (sub-A page 79, 5 Apr 1950) — first FBI-corpus retention of a sitting US Representative claiming a UAP sighting; Frank Ryman Coast Guardsman Seattle photograph (sub-A page 123, 5 Jul 1947) — earliest item in sub-A; Carl Jung Alamogordo APRO/Lorenzen release (29 Jul 1958, sub-A page 4); MRS. SAUCER Saginaw MI 7-witness police-station UAP (30 Jul 1957). Strict 15-page cap honored: 8 NEW + 6 UPDATED = 14. New: references/fbi-hq-62-83894-sub-a (master TABLE + ~50 catalogued items + press-shadow-vs-independent fractions + cross-cuts), entities/nicap (NICAP entity), entities/delmer-s-fahrney (RAdm Fahrney entity), references/sighting-caldwell-gray-goose-1949-08 (Caldwell AF-resolution event), references/sighting-killian-dc6-1959-02-24 (Killian 35-passenger DC-6), references/sighting-schmidt-kearney-1957-11-05 (Schmidt felony-record contactee), references/drew-pearson-saucers-nil-1950-11-25 (Pearson confidential-AF-files column), references/sighting-quantico-marines-1954-01 (Quantico DC-7-navigation-lights misidentification). Updated: entities/fbi-hq-62-83894-file (sub-A row + HEADLINE on press-clippings appendix + channel (a) refined to industrial scale + first-sub-file-in-corpus + queued count 6→5), entities/donald-keyhoe (sub-A continuation 1953-1959 NICAP director-of-investigations role), concepts/aztec-saucer-myth-1950 (channel ε via Drew Pearson Nov 1950), concepts/saucer-as-hoax-default (refined typology with three-branch resolution: hoax / natural-phenomena misidentification / NEW misidentification of normal aircraft + Caldwell pre-saucer-era experimental-aviation residue + Schmidt felony-record contactee), concepts/civilian-saucer-hypothesis-1947 (press-shadow note on Russian-tanker Frame 2: sub-A page 113-114 retains LA Examiner article that section 1 serial 13 received via Milwaukee Sentinel — confirms sub-A is press-shadow of main file), index.md (entities +2 = nicap + delmer-s-fahrney; references +6 = fbi-hq-62-83894-sub-a + sighting-caldwell-gray-goose-1949-08 + sighting-killian-dc6-1959-02-24 + sighting-schmidt-kearney-1957-11-05 + drew-pearson-saucers-nil-1950-11-25 + sighting-quantico-marines-1954-01; FBI HQ 62-HQ-83894 file entry updated to 12-of-17 + sub-A press-shadow). Tag taxonomy: no novel tags introduced. Corpus firsts: first sub-file in the wiki corpus (62-HQ-83894-A Vol 1 Serial 1 OPEN); first preserved press photographs in the corpus (Gray Goose helicopter + Roto-Plane Aug 1949); first ex-Navy-flag-officer publicly endorsing UAP-exceeds-earth-aerodynamics (Fahrney NICAP); first credentialed civilian-research organization (NICAP); first FBI-corpus retention of journalism citing classified AF saucer files (Pearson Nov 1950); first felony-record contactee witness (Schmidt 1957); largest single-event witness population in FBI corpus (Killian DC-6 35 passengers); first sitting US Representative personal-sighting claim (Engel R-MI Apr 1950); first FBI-corpus military-base mass-misidentification of normal civilian aircraft (Quantico Marines DC-7 navigation lights Jan 1954); first explicit Cold-War counter-intel framing of saucer denial (Apr 1950 'Valuable to Russia' clipping). Manifest entry added LAST with content_hash. Series position: 12-of-17 — fifth and last paradigm-defining ingest of the FBI HQ 62-HQ-83894 series; remaining 5 sections (sections 3, 6, 8-10) are still queued."
