- [2026-05-11T02:30:00Z] INGEST source="sources/65_hs1-834228961_62-hq-83894_section_8.json" pages_created=10 pages_updated=5 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official notes="217-OCR-page eighth section of FBI HQ 62-HQ-83894 (serials 344-384, 22 Jul 1954 - 7 Nov 1957). SHA-256 e06eb132faf5d4ee5e9e7f4c0d06cf63fae050776e824f70e8fb2f810aecb0cd; size_bytes=291179. Section 8 is the post-Robertson-Panel passive-accumulation tranche (longest single-section span in wiki at ~39 months). Three structurally novel paradigm events: (1) FRANCES SWAN PSYCHIC-CHANNELING CASE (serial 346, 9 Aug 1954) — first FBI-corpus channeling case with retired Adm Herbert B. Knowles routing through Sen Margaret Chase Smith → SecDef → President; Canadian Project Magnet physicist Wilbur Smith unofficially present at Knowles residence 24-26 Jul 1954 with BuAer Hutson; FBI WFO interview at Pentagon Room 2912 on 2 Aug 1954; (2) SEN. RUSSELL USSR SIGHTING FBI/CIA/IAC handling (serials 358 + 361 + 862, Oct-Nov 1955) — Allen Dulles 18 Oct 1955 IAC executive-session SUPPRESSION DECISION (the matter should be kept quiet to avoid Soviet concern about US travel rights), Dr Francis Clauser CIA OSI debrief 27 Oct 1955 at Russell Washington residence, Herbert Scoville Jr 2-page TS memo to IAC distribution list (TS Control 115485 Series A Copy 6 of 10) finding evidence not sufficiently firm for novel-aircraft conclusion; (3) NSC-ROUTED KRASUSKI CASE (serials 382-384, 4-15 Nov 1957) — J. Patrick Coyne NSC → A. H. Belmont FBI Asst Director memorandum → Hoover URGENT teletype → Detroit interview by SA Cassius Rathbun → 6-recipient inter-agency cc (State Office of Security, CIA Deputy Director Plans, Army G-2, ONI, OSI/AF, INS), NEW intake channel (k) growing inventory from 10 to 11 channels. Plus: Stringfield CRIFO 4-serial thread (349/350/376/377) including private 21 Sep 1954 Lt Col O Mara Wright Field interview + Eva Reich orgone-energy OROP Desert Ea letter; Detroit Flying Saucer Club ESPIONAGE-X (serials 348/356/357) with Bureau formal 18 Jan 1955 instruction to SAC Detroit affirming AF-saucer-jurisdiction; APRO first FBI-corpus reference (serial 369, Sep 1956 Lorenzen Alamogordo NM via Wold McDonnell Aircraft St. Louis tip); NACA Dryden + Doolittle flat saucer-denial to House Appropriations (serial 374, Feb 1957); Sylvia Richards FBI-employee + NSA-fiance Henderson NC sighting (serials 364-365, Apr 1956); Houston Blevins fallen-metallic-ash physical-trace second case in corpus (serial 380, Oct 1957); Charles Yost OC Research Lab St. Clair Shores Michigan electric-fields civilian-research (serial 358) — first by-name FBI-corpus reference to the Canadian Project (Project Magnet). 10 NEW pages: references/fbi-hq-62-83894-section-8 + concepts/psychic-channeling-uap + references/sighting-frances-swan-1954-07 + entities/frances-swan + entities/herbert-b-knowles + entities/wilbur-b-smith + entities/leonard-h-stringfield + entities/civilian-research-interplanetary-flying-objects + entities/aerial-phenomena-research-organization + concepts/cia-iac-uap-engagement. 5 UPDATED pages: entities/fbi-hq-62-83894-file (added section 8 row + new HEADLINE + new channel k + section-8 see-also batch); references/sighting-trans-caucasus-1955-10-04 (added FBI/CIA/IAC handling); references/fbi-detroit-100-26505-krasuski-1957 (added HQ-side cross-filing 62-83894 serials 382-384); entities/wladyslaw-krasuski (added HQ-side filing); concepts/contactee-movement-1952-1954 (added section 8 extension). 13 of 17 artifacts in 62-HQ-83894 now ingested. Strict 15-page cap honored."

- [2026-05-10T22:50:00Z] INGEST source="sources/65_hs1-834228961_62-hq-83894_section_3.json" pages_created=11 pages_updated=7 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official notes="190-OCR-page third section of FBI HQ 62-HQ-83894 (serials 101-130, ~14 Aug - 9 Oct 1947). SHA-256 46cdab03219483eb15bf2ae93046bfa8154d871fb151278e88c5565f0898ef55; size_bytes=308432. Paradigm-defining institutional-decision content: (1) THE BB-57 BIRTH CHAIN — three primary-source documents at 62-83894-121-122: 3 Sep 1947 ADC D333.5EX letter by Col R H Smith By Command of Stratemeyer (the AAF-side provocation, ash can covers/toilet seats framing); 25 Sep 1947 Ladd → Hoover memo with Hoover handwritten I agree 9-27-47 + A proposed Bulletin is attached for your approval (the literal birth document of BB-57; drafted by SA Reynolds SWR:AJB); 27 Sep 1947 Hoover → Maj Gen George C. McDonald letter (the FBI external-facing termination announcement 4 days before BB-57 issues internally). (2) EARLIEST BY-NAME OPERATION MOGUL REFERENCE in the wiki UAP corpus — 23 Sep 1947 Fitch → Ladd memo (62-83894-122) on Watskeka IL Whedon recovered instrument; Mrs. Whedon's Mogul-attribution investigated and explicitly DENIED by Wright Field T-2 + Major Hopkins (radio/technical-experience expert) + Lt Col George Garrett; classified as hoax (old-type radio loud speaker, 1910/1916 Nathaniel Baldwin patent dates). (3) FBI SEATTLE MAURY ISLAND COMPLETE REPORT — 18 Aug 1947 R H Fletcher → Director letter (62-83894-110, OCR pp. 34-58) including verbatim 7 Aug 1947 Crisman+Dahl signed admission to SA David A. MacCulloch that the disc-explosion narrative was constructed at Ray Palmer/Venture Press telephonic request; Arnold paid by Palmer to come to Tacoma; B-25 crash forensically attributed to engine failure (no sabotage); CLOSES long-pending open thread on the existing Maury Island sighting page. (4) FBI's OWN-CHANNEL ARNOLD DEPOSITION — 19 Aug 1947 SA Joseph E. Jette Boise Resident Agency interview (62-83894-111, OCR pp. 88-110) preserving the 26 Jun 1947 Palmer-to-Arnold letter as documentary evidence and Arnold's first-person Mt. Rainier narrative + frustration with institutional non-engagement. (5) PRE-COOPERATION-REGIME SCHULGEN BACKGROUND-INVESTIGATION REQUEST — 20 Aug 1947 Fitch → Ladd memo (62-83894-116) — Schulgen requests subversive-background and ulterior-motive investigations on Byron B. Savage (RCA Field Engineer, OKC) and Kenneth Arnold (Boise); the earliest documented Schulgen institutional channel to the FBI on specific witness-subjects, structurally distinct from the famous 5 Sep 1947 formal denial letter. (6) URIE SNAKE RIVER CANYON SIGHTING — 13 Aug 1947 ~1 PM A. C. Urie + 8yo + 10yo sons (62-83894-115); Mormon Poplars 'spun around on top as if they were in a vacuum' aerodynamic-effect signature. (7) MUROC 4AF-channel sworn affidavits — 4AF-1208-I 18 Aug 1947 (OCR pp. 60-72) preserving 6 sworn affidavits (Gilkey/Shoop/Ruvolo/McHenry/Wise/Stapp) parallel to SIGN Inc 1/1c/1e/2/4 intake. (8) FINAL POST-BB-57 datapoint — 9 Oct 1947 Reynolds → Ladd memo (62-83894-130) showing AF Intelligence (Lt Col K.L./G.D. Garrett OCR ambiguity) transmitting photographs to FBI for completion of Bureau files 8 days post-termination. 11 NEW pages: references/fbi-hq-62-83894-section-3 + references/fbi-ladd-proposed-bulletin-1947-09-25 + references/fbi-hoover-mcdonald-letter-1947-09-27 + references/adc-d333.5ex-cooperation-1947-09-03 + references/fbi-fitch-ladd-mogul-1947-09-23 + concepts/operation-mogul + references/fbi-seattle-maury-island-report-1947-08-18 + references/fbi-arnold-boise-interview-1947-08-19 + references/fbi-schulgen-savage-arnold-background-1947-08-20 + references/sighting-snake-river-canyon-1947-08-13 + (one slot under cap). 7 UPDATED pages: references/fbi-bureau-bulletin-57-1947 (substantial promotion of inferred to extracted; closes originator open thread) + concepts/fbi-aaf-saucer-cooperation-1947 (adds 6 new timeline rows including the BB-57 birth chain) + entities/fbi-hq-62-83894-file (major HEADLINE addition; section 3 added; ingestion count 13→14) + references/sighting-maury-island-1947-06-21 (closes FBI Seattle complete report open thread) + entities/g-d-garrett (adds 23 Sep 1947 Mogul-denial datapoint + 9 Oct 1947 photograph-transmittal datapoint) + references/sighting-muroc-1947-07-08 (adds 4AF-channel parallel sworn affidavits material) + index.md. 14 of 17 artifacts in 62-HQ-83894 now ingested. Remaining: sections 6, 9, 10. Strict 15-page cap honored (11 created)."

- [2026-05-11T03:30:00Z] INGEST source="sources/65_hs1-834228961_62-hq-83894_section_9.json" pages_created=8 pages_updated=10 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official notes="290-OCR-page ninth section of FBI HQ 62-HQ-83894 (serials 385-447, 14 Nov 1957 → 21 Jul 1966). SHA-256 c83a702f1a89052d035b5b2d4a2724290ceafc571235232fd6e1e4c0e552d532; size_bytes=350966. The longest single-section span in the file (~8 years 8 months). Five paradigm-defining strands: (1) ROACH→BELMONT SPUTNIK MEMO serial 387 (12 Nov 1957) — Ever since the Russians released Sputnik framing of the Vernal UT/Nebraska/Albany→Miami/Coast Guard/Levelland surge as an AF-disposed non-authentic mass; the canonical FBI-side primary source for the post-Sputnik UAP-report reactivation; cited as 11-12-57 AF assurance in every subsequent annual-review memo through 1962. (2) MIB / THREE-MEN-IN-BLACK MYTH FILED BACK INTO THE FILE — Marck letter + Phoenix interview (serials 397/398, Dec 1958), Maney via Saucerian Bulletin Vol 3 #4 retention (serial 396, Dec 1958, Adamski-3-men-of-FBI-AF-Intel-CIA quote), Montello direct inquiry (serial 437, Jan 1964), Chicago SAC mailing of Barker book to HQ (serial 403, Feb 1959 — distinct retention from the existing serial-403 jacket-clipping wiki anchor); the FBI never investigates Bender or rebuts the rumor. (3) ANNUAL POLICY-STASIS REVIEW MEMOS — Sanders 392 (Oct 1958, the canonical 4-paragraph history compiling BB-42 + 9-3-47 ADC + BB-57 + 9-27-47 Hoover-McDonald + SAC Letter 38 + SAC Letter 83 + 11-12-57 AF assurance), Donahoe 422 (Sep 1960), Donahoe 425 (Oct 1961), Wannall 427 (Oct 1962), each closing for your information. There appears to be no necessity for additional instructions; chain disappears after Wannall 1962, plausibly because the Apr 1964 Socorro case made the only one complaint claim untenable. (4) SOCORRO NM ZAMORA LANDING CASE filed in sequential 62-HQ-83894 — serials 435 (27 Apr 1964 Albuquerque teletype + La Madera NM Orlando Gallegos parallel sighting 26 Apr 1964 ~150 mi N of Socorro with matching butane-tank/blue-white-flame/rectangular-V-indentation profile), 436 (28 Apr 1964 HQ → Albuquerque airtel containing canonical Director hedge Our policy is not to investigate but assure that Air Force is cognizant), 438 (8 May 1964 Byrnes report — also retained as the wiki standalone anchor); confirms BB-57 policy stasis held even for the file most kinematically anomalous case. (5) LARRY W. BRYANT ARC — Army civilian / editorial clerk Fort Eustis VA, born 23 Feb 1938; first FBI letter Feb 1959 (serial 402); 1963 follow-up triggered Hoover Gandy refusal (serial 430, 27 Sep 1963) + Director airtel to SAC Norfolk (serial 431) + Second Army G-2 background check; 1965 Lets Challenge the UFO Censors article retained at serial 439. Plus secondary content: Joseph Perry Grand Blanc photo case (serials 410-416 + 419 + 445, multi-year through Apr 1966); George Van Tassel Phipps Auditorium Denver talk (serial 418, May 1960); Frank Stranges Seattle thread (serials 423-424, Oct 1960); Stringfield CRIFO continuation (serial 426, Oct 1961); Hoover first explicit FBI alien-body-custody denial the FBI has never had custody of an occupant from a foreign planet (serial 447, 21 Jul 1966 to Steven G. Gray Cleburne TX). 8 NEW pages: references/fbi-hq-62-83894-section-9 + references/fbi-roach-belmont-sputnik-memo-1957-11-12 + references/sighting-grand-blanc-perry-1960 + concepts/three-men-in-black + concepts/sputnik-saucer-reactivation-1957 + concepts/fbi-saucer-policy-stasis-1958-1962 + entities/albert-k-bender + entities/larry-w-bryant. 10 UPDATED pages: references/fbi-hq-62-83894-section-8 (closes section-9-picks-up-post-Sputnik open question CLOSED CHECK) + references/sighting-socorro-nm-1964-04-24 (adds section-9 multi-serial filing context + La Madera NM parallel sighting) + references/fbi-hq-62-83894-serial-438 (notes section-9 cross-retention + Director hedge) + references/barker-1956-they-knew-too-much (adds Feb 1959 Chicago SAC mailing of book copy) + entities/gray-barker (adds Saucerian Bulletin Vol 3 #4 + book-mailing) + entities/international-flying-saucer-bureau (adds Bender silencing rumor downstream filings) + entities/fbi-hq-62-83894-file (15-of-17 ingested; channel inventory 11→12; section 9 HEADLINE) + concepts/engine-stall-phenomenon (notes section-9 Roach-memo HQ summary anchor for Levelland) + projects/uap/uap (section 9 entry under FBI HQ archives) + index.md. 15 of 17 artifacts in 62-HQ-83894 now ingested. Remaining: sections 6, 10. Strict 15-page cap honored (8 created)."

- [2026-05-10T00:00:00Z] INGEST source="sources/65_hs1-834228961_62-hq-83894_section_10.json" pages_created=8 pages_updated=7 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official notes="184-OCR-page tenth and final-spanning section of FBI HQ 62-HQ-83894 (serials 448-486, 6 Sep 1966 → 14 Jun 1977). SHA-256 5cab1c8931c722517ebf32c73d5107f4ff03c916c07dc417e5a089fe5519cbf2; size_bytes=368516. The file's longest single span (~10 years 9 months) and effective terminus pending the missing section 6. Five paradigm-defining strands: (1) PROJECT BLUE BOOK 17 DEC 1969 TERMINATION filed back into the file 7+ years later via McClanahan-Moore exchange (serials 483-484, Jan 1977) — the 14 Jan 1977 USAF UFO Fact Sheet by Lt Col H. A. McClanahan (USAF Office of Information) is the canonical FBI-side primary-source artifact, preserving verbatim the three Blue Book conclusions (no national-security indication, no anomalous-technology, no ETI indication) + 1947-1969 sighting totals chart + post-closure NARA transfer disposition + Condon Report ordering info (contract F44620-67-C-0035, AD 680:975-977); the 24 Jan 1977 FBI reply by Donald W. Moore Jr. (External Affairs AD) thanks McClanahan for apprising us of the termination of your project — phrasing that implies the FBI had no formal channel notification of the 1969 closure until 1977. (2) CONDON COMMITTEE 1966-1968 enters the file as press echo only — Newsweek clippings at serials 451 (31 Oct 1966 issue, $300K contract award) + 455 (23 Jan 1967 issue, study-launch + Klass/Keyhoe/Hopkinsville coverage), LA Times wire at 456 (24 Jan 1967, landing-prediction letter Condon received), NICAP-Colorado-Project mention in Edwards subcommittee letter at 465 (20 Nov 1967); no actual Condon Report (Jan 1969, 1465pp) filed in section 10. (3) CARTER-ERA WHITE HOUSE COORDINATION QUESTION — 14 Jun 1977 OSTP Asst Director Stanley Schneider phones FBI Asst Director Jay Cochran Jr. (Tech Services) asking on Press Secretary Jody Powell's behalf whether any Executive-Branch coordination on UFO information exists post-Blue-Book; same-day Cochran-McDermott internal memo (16 cc copies, broadest distribution of any UAP memo in section 10) + 15 Jun reply enclosing Feb 1975 FBI Law Enforcement Bulletin UFO article (article itself not retained); first post-Blue-Book Executive-Branch coordination question + strongest 1977 statement of FBI no conceivable jurisdiction disclaimer; file's effective last filing at serial 486. (4) MIB MYTH MATURES INTO REFLEXIVE CITIZEN-FBI QUERIES — Glenn Evans Waterloo IL 14 Mar 1968 (serial 466) asks does the FBI really have a saucer man, Larry Stephens Del City OK 14 May 1969 (serial 473) writes a structurally complete inventory of 1969 MIB folklore (oriental features + dark clothes + black cars + run-down attempts + mail-tampering + telephone taps + photographs of UFO-witness homes), three Canadian children Brassington/Smith/Hargreaves Ajax Ontario 8 Oct 1969 (serial 475) ask if the magazine photo of two FBI agents leading a person from outer space down the street is genuine; same magazine photo (originated in German press) circulates internationally; FBI replies deny photo represents employees of this Bureau without engaging the underlying MIB claim. (5) TWO NEW INTAKE CHANNELS — (l) Post-Blue-Book Fact-Sheet enclosure routing the 1969 termination back into the file via 1977 AF transmittal, and (m) White-House coordination inquiry via EOP/OSTP → FBI; channel inventory grows from 12 to 14. Plus secondary paradigm content: Peyerl Black-Forest WW2 saucer disclosure (serial 460, 26 Apr 1967 Miami walk-in, 21-foot radio-controlled saucer-shaped aircraft attributed to engineer KUEHR with N₂H₄O/CH₃OH fuel-chemistry data, motivated by Vietnam-utility framing); Dallas Anonymous Informant ETI claims (serial 464, 9 Oct 1967 walk-in by anonymous young white female, in-person human-form ETI contact Jul 1967 + ETI departed 21 Aug 1967 + 4 specific UAP claims including DEW Line shootdown); Forrest Wood ex-CIA self-claim phone call 12:50am intoxicated (serial 472, 16 May 1969); Milwaukee fallen object NMCC inquiry (serial 482, 22 Aug 1974, 13×8×5 metallic jagged object with internal heat source recovered by Milwaukee Police, second physical-object recovery in FBI corpus + first NMCC-to-FBI direct routing); Bryant 1974 records-request (serial 481, 31 Mar 1974) confirms 17 Sep 1963 Castles interview date — closes section-9 open thread; Dave T. Ozanne impostor case (serial 479, Mar 1973, civil impersonation of USAF Captain inquiring at Waco TX newspaper); UFO CONTACT igap journal Vol 2 No 5 (serial 462, ~50 OCR pages of Adamski-continuation magazine printed in Denmark, Steckling Belgium tour Sep 1966, Rodeffer NASA 22-officials meeting, Open Letter to Queen Juliana); J. - [2026-05-10T21:25:00Z] INGEST source="sources/65_hs1-834228961_62-hq-83894_section_6.json" pages_created=12 pages_updated=6 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official notes="271-OCR-page sixth and FINAL-of-17 section of FBI HQ 62-HQ-83894 (serials 246-301, ~21 Aug 1950 → ~15 Aug 1952). SHA-256 3f17bde6c1ea7554338277a1b09def76bc8ed06676b8cd1fd267b568fd42c5d2; size_bytes=407993. FOIPA reference 99-3067 (distinct from prior tranches); tag 8/11/1274188 (Aug-2012 release-tranche). The section bridges section-5 terminus (8 Sep 1950) and section-7 start (9 Aug 1952), closing the largest remaining temporal gap in the wiki's FBI HQ corpus. With section 6 ingested, the file's 17-artifact wiki ingest is COMPLETE. Five paradigm-defining strands: (1) PROJECT TWINKLE FBI-canonized via the 21 Aug 1950 Belmont→Ladd memo (serial 246) + 9 Oct 1950 Ladd→Director update (serial 250) — AMC Geophysical Research Division (Cambridge MA) → Land-Air Inc (Alamogordo NM); Dr Anthony O. Mirarchi as Project Engineer; 24-hour day watch at Vaughn NM; first operational success 24 May 1950 with 8-10 objects; FBI Albuquerque plugged into Mirarchi's reporting chain via espionage/sabotage-jurisdiction tripwire; the first FBI-corpus formal AF-contractor critical-facility scientific-study program. (2) LAPAZ SEVENTH REPORT (23 May 1950) — 11-criteria taxonomy distinguishing green fireballs from typical meteors (horizontal trajectory + low altitude + sub-meteor velocity + noiselessness + no progressive brightening + N-half-sky approach + meteor-shower temporal association + 5218Å copper-salt spectrum + 2-3 sec duration + horizontal infall + 2030 local-time max-frequency coincides w/ Urals daytime 0700-1300) + the Urals-missile fallback hypothesis (<15-min flight time from southern Urals to NM); LaPaz's two-category interpretation: ~50% meteoric + ~50% US guided-missile testing; recommendations (dual-station Roswell+Sacramento optical + Stanford Manning radar + NM School of Mines Crozier dust collection) operationally implemented as Twinkle 12 weeks later. (3) CABELL AFOIC-CC-1 DIRECTIVE (8 Sep 1950) — AIRMEM-#4 (Feb 1949) successor by same author, same office (HQ USAF D/I), elaborated 9-element EEI schema, AF Form 112 follow-up within 10 days, MCIS routing destination (vs AIRMEM-#4's MCIAXO-3), explicit publicity-suppression clause, FBI Director on info-copy distribution (with G-2 Army + DNI + USCG INT + State R&I + CIA truncated); structurally parallel to FBI's BB-57→SAC#38→SAC#83 cadence; channel (e) strengthened. (4) FORT MONMOUTH RADAR (10-11 Sep 1951, Newark SAC URGENT 20 Sep 1951) — Andrew J. Reid G-2 Ft Monmouth reports AN/MPG-1 + SCR-584 radar tracking targets that exceeded the 700 mph aided-tracking limit; vertical-rise + azimuth-overrun + signal-falloff-on-maneuver signature; three witnesses at events 2+3; Army G-2 → FBI Newark direct channel extension. (5) OAK RIDGE NEPA / 663rd AC&W SEQUENCE (Dec 1950 - Jan 1951) — 18 Dec 1950 Lt Col Hood USAF + Cdr Hribar USN + Maj Steele USAF + Maj Carss USAF + civilians Gray + Frey + NEPA personnel Miller/Calkins/Coneybear/Mooneyham/Bly visual + 663rd AC&W radar (McGhee Tyson) tracking 0832-0845; 20 Dec 1950 + 14 Dec 1950 + 16 Jan 1951 follow-on radar events; Hoover's 5 Dec 1950 personal directive investigating 'radar jamming by ionization of particles in atmosphere' over Oak Ridge (original copy filed in 65-58300-141 Vital Installations); 8 Dec 1950 FBI Richmond URGENT documenting Army Intelligence Richmond high-alert. Additional paradigm content: STEWART/TYLER RITCHIE HIGHWAY 29 Mar 1952 — first FBI-corpus EM-engine-stoppage car-encounter case + 50-ft saucer + vacuum-cleaner sound + 'wiring magnetized' candidate mechanism + hammer-and-sickle decal background subtext + Tyler retraction; SAVANNAH RIVER PLANT 10 May 1952 — first SRP UAP event (predates canonical 8 Aug 1952 by 3 months), 4 du Pont witnesses, 8 disc-shaped objects across 4 clusters over 30 min, 'all agencies are conducting no inquiry'; MENZEL LOOK MAGAZINE DEBUNK (17 Jun 1952) — first FBI-corpus Harvard-astrophysicist temperature-inversion-haze + atmospheric-illusion framework; RHODES/PEARSON/BLUMENTHAL CHAIN-OF-CUSTODY DISPUTE (Jun 1952) — first FBI-corpus UAP-photograph chain-of-custody dispute, OSI Barnard+Neville+AF Intel Young joint denial of Blumenthal contact, Rhodes's narrative drift from voluntary surrender (1947) to FBI borrowed (1952); FRANK SCULLY COMMUNIST-BACKGROUND-CHECK (Oct 1950 serials 253-254) — third file-recategorization in corpus (62-83894-254 → 100-2244-6, 31 Oct 1950); HITCHENS WINCHESTER NEW HAVEN sphere sighting (Oct 1950); EEKHOUT MOUTON & CO NETHERLANDS ionization-disc walk-in (May 1952); McINDOO THESSALON LAKE ONTARIO CANADA case (Jul 1952) — first FBI-RCMP UAP routing; WILSON LOOK ONR-Skyhook-balloon-debunk article (Jan 1951); SUNDAY GRAPHIC LINKE German-saucer-takeoff article (Jul 1952); CINCINNATI ENQUIRER RIEDEL V-weapon German letter (Aug 1952); OSI PHILADELPHIA MAJOR ELDRIDGE 'no longer interested' thread (Aug 1952) — immediate precipitating context for SAC Letter #83 issued 2 weeks later. Intake-channel inventory UNCHANGED at 14 — section 6 strengthens channel (e) (via Cabell AFOIC-CC-1) and channel (c) (via Stewart Ritchie Highway OSI file) without adding a structurally novel channel. FILE-COMPLETE: 17-of-17 artifacts ingested; total OCR pages across 10 sections = 2,159; bounds 6 Jul 1947 → 14 Jun 1977 (~30 years), unchanged from section-10. 12 NEW pages: references/fbi-hq-62-83894-section-6 + concepts/project-twinkle + references/lapaz-seventh-report-1950-05-23 + references/cabell-afoic-cc-1-unconventional-aircraft-1950-09-08 + references/belmont-ladd-aerial-phenomena-nm-1950-08-21 + references/osi-17th-district-nm-summary-1950-05-25 + references/sighting-fort-monmouth-radar-1951-09 + references/sighting-oak-ridge-nepa-1950-12-1951-01 + references/sighting-stewart-ritchie-highway-1952-03-29 + references/sighting-savannah-river-plant-1952-05-10 + references/menzel-look-flying-saucers-1952-06 + references/rhodes-pearson-photos-1952-06. 6 UPDATED pages: entities/fbi-hq-62-83894-file (17-of-17 FILE COMPLETE + section 6 HEADLINE + full structural-arc table + final-totals) + entities/lincoln-la-paz (adds 23 May 1950 Seventh Report + Project Twinkle institutional canonization + WWII OSRD/Second AF credentials + voluntary-consultant labor-economics) + concepts/green-fireballs-new-mexico (adds Project Twinkle establishment + LaPaz Seventh Report institutional response + 14 Oct 1949 Los Alamos follow-on conference documentation) + index.md + log.md + hot.md. - [2026-05-11T04:00:00Z] INGEST source="sources/dow-uap-d4-mission-report-arabian-gulf-2020.json" pages_created=1 pages_updated=2 mode=append project=uap source_type=document source_quality=official series=dow-uap series_position=2-of-40 signal_class=navy-mission-report+series-format-anchor notes="1315-byte 5-OCR-page Mistral-OCR'd artifact (SHA-256 beccf4157c52c1407342b107b78fc887b3bb32563a2de090ba750618709052d6; underlying PDF doc_size_bytes 29,121). FIRST SUBSTANTIVE d*-prefixed ingest of the dow-uap FOIA-release series — establishes the structural template for the ~25 mission-report files in the series. Format: pages 0-3 carry only the per-page classification header '# 1.4(a)' (EO 13526 §1.4(a) — military plans/weapons/operations ^[inferred]); page 4 carries the substantive content — a USMTF GENTEXT/UAP message segment with classification banner SECRET/REL TO USA, FVEY (OCR'd as FVEV; Y→V confusion at low DPI ^[inferred]). The encounter datum: AT 1258Z, [REDACTED] OBSERVED POSS UAP IVO 34SDG9041417044; BRIEF OBSERVATION PRECLUDED UAP ALTITUDE ESTIMATES; VELOCITY ESTIMATED AT 321 KNOTS; UAP INCREASED SPEED AND CHANGED DIRECTION TOWARDS THE EAST. PILOT identity redacted (b)(6); second (b)(6) line plausibly co-witness or airframe-ID. HEADLINE 1 — MISSION-REPORT FORMAT TEMPLATE: per-page '# 1.4(a)' header + USMTF GENTEXT/UAP segment + (b)(6) pilot redaction + SECRET/REL TO USA, FVEY banner + MGRS coordinates + knots velocity + Zulu time without calendar date inside the substantive page (filename year is the only date anchor ^[inferred]). Expected to recur across remaining ~24 mission-report files; to be validated and revised in place. HEADLINE 2 — FILENAME-VS-INTERNAL-COORDINATE GEOGRAPHIC MISMATCH: the filename labels theater 'Arabian Gulf' but the MGRS 34SDG9041417044 decodes as UTM zone 34 / band S, which covers the Eastern Mediterranean / Aegean / NE Libya / Egypt (~18°-24°E, 32°-40°N) — the Arabian Gulf falls in UTM zones 39-40 (~48°-57°E). Three candidate reconciliations all ^[inferred]: (a) OCR misread on zone digits; (b) curator-applied filename mislabels theater; (c) vessel-or-airframe in Med, operationally-grouped-with-Arabian-Gulf. No source-side evidence resolves which. CORPUS-LEVEL OBSERVATION RECORDED: curator-applied filename theater labels in dow-uap- series may not match internal-document coordinates — to be re-checked against the next d* ingest. HEADLINE 3 — BEHAVIORAL CLASS: the record is brief-observation-class (kinematics-only — velocity + course change), NOT engagement-class. No close approach, no co-location, no cross-aircraft target selection, no morphology, no sustained observation. Captured as a NEGATIVE DATUM on concepts/uap-aircraft-engagement: modern Navy mission-report corpus produces brief-observation-class records by default; engagement-class events should remain a small minority. 1 NEW page: references/dow-uap-d4-mission-arabian-gulf-2020 (the artifact itself, with full format-observations + coordinate-vs-filename ambiguity). 2 UPDATED pages: entities/dow-uap-foia-release (Mission-report format section added; filename-vs-coordinate discrepancy recorded; 2-of-40 ingested) + concepts/uap-aircraft-engagement (Negative datum subsection added). 3 STATE FILES UPDATED: index.md (+1 entry under references) + log.md + hot.md + .manifest.json. The originating unit, calendar date, and definitive theater are NOT recoverable from this OCR pass; deferred to follow-on ingests. Strict 15-page cap honored (1 NEW, 2 UPDATED — well under cap; series-format anchor doesn't require many pages on its own)."

(1) Inc 112 cluster (~p34-47): the existing master-table row recorded only the 1-witness Ashley OH Paines/Slack sighting; the source actually contains a 7-witness 2-town simultaneity cluster — Ashley OH (Paines/Slack + Stephens, 1400) and Delaware OH (Mrs Stephenson + Rev Stephenson + Mrs Bergen + Rev Bergen + Mrs Kirkpatrick, 1430). (2) Inc 130 (~p86-89): the existing master-table row recorded witnesses as N/S; the source contains a rich industrial-witness case — W.A. Bonneville, Territory Manager B.F. Goodrich Company, Bismarck ND, 20-min field-glass observation 17 May 1948 ~2330 between Plevna and Miles City MT at the Powder River Bridge of a fast bright-white ball performing arcs. The source also preserves a 23 May 1948 letter from Bonneville to US Army Intelligence Div Washington and a 18 June 1948 reply detailing answers to a 15 June 1948 follow-up letter from Col W.R. Clingerman at Wright Field carrying 'Refer - MCIAXO-3' — the earliest documented MCIAXO-3 routing line in the wiki corpus, pushing the prior 1949 anchor back ~6 months and identifying Clingerman as the named officer. (3) Inc 151+152 (~p128-136): the existing master-table row for 151 recorded Toney+Huggins as witnesses but missed the morphology-sketch + investigator-signature detail; the source contains hand-signed sketches and location diagrams by 1st Lt Russell J. Carey Jr., Base Intelligence Officer 331st AFBU Reserve Training Stout Field Indianapolis, for both Inc 151 (Toney/Huggins broad-short-propeller-with-cups, NE Indianapolis 29 Jul 0955) and Inc 152 (Swigert cymbal 20-ft white flat disc, S-Central Indianapolis 31 Jul 0825 — two-window sight-line verification, same-day callout). Carey is the only AMC field-intel officer in the bundle's central section who personally signs hand-drawn morphology illustrations. 4 NEW pages: references/sighting-delaware-ashley-oh-1948-04 + references/sighting-bonneville-bfg-1948-05 + references/sighting-indianapolis-1948-07 + entities/russell-j-carey. 2 UPDATED pages: references/project-sign-incident-summaries-101-172 (master-table rows for Inc 112/130/151/152 expanded; 'dedicated wiki pages' count bumped from 4 to 7; Named Officers section adds Clingerman + Carey + Hoskins + Bonneville + Stephenson + Bergen + Stephens; Cross-References + See Also sections add 3 new sighting pages + Carey entity) + entities/air-materiel-command (MCIAXO-3 routing-codes row updated — earliest documented use moved from 1949 to 15 June 1948 with Col W.R. Clingerman as the named officer; cross-link to new sighting-bonneville-bfg-1948-05 page). 4 STATE FILES UPDATED: .manifest.json (existing entry expanded — no duplicate; pages_created +4; pages_updated +2; repaired_at + repair_reason fields added; stats.total_pages 316 → 320; projects.uap.last_ingested updated) + index.md (4 new entries: 1 entity + 3 references) + log.md (this entry) + hot.md. Cross-references `PREVIOUSLY REPORTED UAP FROM 48FW` — second USAF-unit anchor in the series after d52's `15 AF / DET 1` internal-originating-unit; 48FW = USAF 48th Fighter Wing (RAF Lakenheath, F-15E Strike Eagle). 1 NEW page: references/dow-uap-d7-mission-arabian-gulf-2020. 2 UPDATED pages: entities/dow-uap-foia-release (5-of-40 count; mission-report template revised at N=3 with d7-introduced + d7-revised elements; filename-vs-internal-document section updated with d7 untestable-bearing-only case + extended date-axis unreliability; multi-service section anchored at 2 USAF unit references) + concepts/uap-aircraft-engagement (engagement axis SPLIT into UAP-toward-aircraft + aircraft-toward-UAP classes; new aircraft-toward-UAP engagement-pipeline section anchored at d7; three sub-classes within brief-observation: kinematic-anomaly + steady-state-cruise + d7's new prosaic-candidate-wind-borne-drift). STRUCTURAL FIRSTS introduced by d7: (1) first morphology descriptor in the `d*` mission-report corpus — `LOOKS LIKE A BALLOON`; (2) first explicit cross-report citation in the `d*` corpus — `PREVIOUSLY REPORTED UAP FROM 48FW`; (3) first fire-control-pipeline UAP datum — WQT + NTS + TFLIR; (4) first aircraft-toward-UAP engagement-pipeline datum in the entire wiki corpus; (5) first explicit USMTF named-field bullets (`UAP Description:` + `Gentext (UAP Event Description):`) vs single free-text Description bullet of d4/d5; (6) first non-`(S/REL)` portion-marking — `(SECRET)` only (no foreign-release authorization); (7) first bearing-only position anchor (`IVO 323'S`) with no MGRS; (8) first MSL (mean sea level) altitude reference (31,000 ft MSL) vs FL pressure-altitude of d5-A; (9) first `d*` mission-report with no internal time anchor (neither Zulu nor calendar date); (10) first wind-coupled velocity descriptor (`TRAVELING WITH THE WINDS`) vs knots-quantified velocity of d4/d5. STATE FILES UPDATED: .manifest.json + index.md + log.md (this entry) + hot.md. MANIFEST DISCIPLINE: new source entry; no duplicates. SIGNAL CLASS: engagement-pipeline (aircraft-side) + prosaic-candidate (UAP-side). THEATER ANCHOR: bearing-only (UNDECIDABLE from internal evidence; filename Arabian Gulf 2020 unverifiable). MULTI-SERVICE PATTERN: USAF anchor count rises from 1 (d52) to 2 (d52 internal + d7 cross-reference); Navy internal-unit anchor count remains 0 at N=5 ingests."
